     1	\section{Decomposing the Wall Clock}
     2	\label{sec:model}
     3	
     4	\subsection{Two components}
     5	
     6	We split the wall clock at every sampled instant according to whether the
     7	engine has any request in flight:
     8	\begin{equation}
     9	  W \;=\; W_{\text{busy}} \;+\; W_{\text{idle}} .
    10	  \label{eq:split}
    11	\end{equation}
    12	Fig.~\ref{fig:decomp} shows the split for every completed run. Two things are
    13	immediately visible and both matter later.
    14	
    15	First, idle time is not noise. It ranges from 7.4\% of the fastest
    16	configuration's sampled span to 36.3\% of the slowest's---the denominator here
    17	is the span the sampler observed, which runs about six minutes longer than the
    18	request wall clock of Table~\ref{tab:runs}, so the fractions against that
    19	wall clock are 7.6\% and 36.7\%---and it is not spread evenly.
    20	Over 1{,}849 stretches the median is 5.6\,s---close to the 5\,s sampling
    21	floor, because most tool calls are quick---but 17.6\% of them exceed 20\,s,
    22	the 90th percentile is 30.1\,s, and the longest is 511\,s
    23	(Fig.~\ref{fig:idle}). The tail, not the middle, is what a scheduler feels,
    24	and it sits at the scale of the 16.85\,s mean tool wait.
    25	
    26	Second, the two components move independently. \texttt{bres}
    27	(\S\ref{sec:results}) reduces idle from 15.9\% to 10.1\%, recovering about 17
    28	minutes---and is nonetheless \emph{slower} than its own baseline, because its
    29	busy time grows by 21 minutes. Any account that treats ``more concurrency'' as
    30	one thing will get this wrong.
    31	
    32	\begin{figure}[tb]
    33	  \centering
    34	  \includegraphics[width=\columnwidth]{figs/fig2_decomp.pdf}
    35	  \caption{Wall clock split into busy and idle for every completed run,
    36	  ordered by total duration. Idle is 7.4\% for the fastest configuration and
    37	  36.3\% for the slowest. Note \texttt{bres}: it cuts idle to 10.1\% and is
    38	  still slower than its own baseline, because busy time grows instead.}
    39	  \label{fig:decomp}
    40	\end{figure}
    41	
    42	\begin{figure}[tb]
    43	  \centering
    44	  \includegraphics[width=\columnwidth]{figs/fig4_idle_segments.pdf}
    45	  \caption{Survival function of idle-stretch duration, pooled over all
    46	  completed runs (1{,}849 stretches). Most stretches are short---the median is
    47	  5.6\,s---but the minutes are not: 69.6\% of all idle \emph{time} sits in
    48	  stretches longer than 20\,s, and the mean stretch is 20.6\,s, the same order
    49	  as the 16.85\,s mean tool wait. The cliff is the 5\,s sampling floor.}
    50	  \label{fig:idle}
    51	\end{figure}
    52	
    53	\subsection{What determines busy time}
    54	
    55	During busy time the engine executes steps; each step produces one token per
    56	running request. Hence
    57	\begin{equation}
    58	  W_{\text{busy}} \;\approx\; \frac{G}{\overline{R}}\,\tau ,
    59	  \label{eq:busy}
    60	\end{equation}
    61	with $G$ the total generated tokens (fixed by the trace at 706{,}010) and
    62	$\tau$ the per-step cost. Across our runs $\tau$ is not a fit parameter we
    63	tuned; we obtain it two independent ways, and reporting both is what shows
    64	they are measuring different things rather than confirming each other.
    65	
    66	\paragraph{Method 1 (derived).} $\tau_1 = W_{\text{busy}} \cdot \overline{R} /
    67	G$. Two choices in that expression are easy to get wrong and both matter. The
    68	time term must be the \emph{busy} component, not the wall clock---using the
    69	wall clock charges idle time to the steps. And $\overline{R}$ must be the mean
    70	running count \emph{during busy time}, not the mean over the whole run: the
    71	whole-run mean includes the idle samples, where the count is zero, so it equals
    72	$\overline{R}_{\text{busy}} \times$ (busy fraction). Using it makes $\tau_1$
    73	too low by exactly the busy fraction---36.3\% for \texttt{cap1}, whose idle
    74	share is 36.3\%. We report both columns in the data, and use the
    75	busy-conditional mean throughout; the whole-run mean is what an earlier
    76	revision of this paper used, and it made the two methods agree far better than
    77	they do.
    78	
    79	\paragraph{Method 2 (independent).} For each step record we compute the
    80	per-request decode interval,
    81	$(\text{total\_duration} - \text{first\_token}) / (\text{output\_len} - 1)$,
    82	and take the median over steps with $\text{output\_len} \ge 32$.
    83	
    84	The two do not agree exactly, and the disagreement is informative rather than
    85	embarrassing. $\tau_1$ exceeds $\tau_2$ on every run, by 7.9--39.8\%, which in
    86	absolute terms is 2.6--8.2\,ms of a 27--37\,ms step. The gap is
    87	\emph{consistent with} engine steps that are prefill and
    88	therefore emit no output token---we can bound it, not identify it, because
    89	subtracting a median from a mean does not isolate a component. All we can form
    90	from these two is their ratio: the excess $\tau_1/\tau_2 - 1$ runs
    91	17.6--39.8\% across the five device-only configurations. Two things follow, and
    92	we separate them. First, it is a ratio of \emph{times}, not of steps, and we
    93	have no measurement of the fraction of step \emph{counts} that are prefill---
    94	chunked prefill makes those two different numbers. Second, even as a time
    95	ratio it is the gap between two measurements, not a decomposition of one: it
    96	is consistent with prefill and non-decode work filling it, but we cannot rule
    97	out that some of the difference is the two estimators disagreeing for other
    98	reasons. So the two are not
    99	two estimates of one number; they are measurements of two different things,
   100	and we do not size a component with their difference. Fig.~\ref{fig:steptime} shows both, run by run. Method 2 remains the one that does not depend on the
   101	quantity it is used to explain, and it is the one we quote when the question
   102	is the cost of a decode step. The engine's own latency counters
   103	(\texttt{itl\_p50}, \texttt{e2e\_p50}, \texttt{ttft\_p50}) are exported as zero
   104	by SGLang v0.5.10 in our configuration, so no directly measured step time
   105	exists in our data; instrumenting this is future work.
   106	
   107	\begin{figure}[tb]
   108	  \centering
   109	  \includegraphics[width=\columnwidth]{figs/fig3_steptime.pdf}
   110	  \caption{Per-step cost by two independent measurements, one run per row:
   111	  circles are model-consistent (all engine steps), crosses are the per-request
   112	  decode interval. They do not agree---$\tau_1$ exceeds $\tau_2$ by 8--40\%---
   113	  and the gap is consistent with the prefill share, not measurement error;
   114	  \S\ref{sec:model} bounds that share rather than identifying it. The dotted line is
   115	  the cost of reading the weights once; every run sits above it.}
   116	  \label{fig:steptime}
   117	\end{figure}
   118	
   119	\subsection{The per-step cost is not constant in concurrency}
   120	
   121	Equation~\ref{eq:busy} would be uninteresting if $\tau$ were a constant. It is
   122	not. Across our 13 runs $\tau_1$ ranges from 27.0 to 36.7\,ms and $\tau_2$
   123	from 20.6 to 33.1\,ms, and both rise with the running count: fitting
   124	Eq.~\ref{eq:elasticity} to the five device-only configurations gives
   125	$\varepsilon = 0.38$ for $\tau_1$ and $0.61$ for $\tau_2$
   126	($R^2 = 0.89$ for the latter, Fig.~\ref{fig:cancel}).
   127	
   128	The relation is a trend, not a law. Two host-tier configurations with similar
   129	running counts behave differently: \texttt{cap4\_hc} runs 1.73 requests at
   130	29.8\,ms while \texttt{F:hz0} runs 1.66 at 35.7\,ms. Running count sets a
   131	floor and a trend, not a value---and on the host-tier family, as we show below,
   132	it does not set the value at all.
   133	
   134	This is the crux of the paper, and we state it at the strength the arithmetic
   135	supports. Hold $\tau_1$ at its value for \texttt{cap1}, 28.8\,ms, and busy
   136	time falls as $1/\overline{R}$: raising the running count from 1.00 to 1.84
   137	should have cut busy time by a factor of 1.84, from 340 minutes to 184. The
   138	measured curve flattens and lands at 234. The rise in $\tau$ takes back 50 of
   139	the 156 minutes that the extra concurrency would otherwise have bought---\emph{a
   140	third} of the gain, not all of it and, as an earlier revision of this paper
   141	claimed, not more than half. The other two thirds are real: raising
   142	concurrency does make the engine faster. What it does not do is make it faster
   143	in proportion, and the shortfall compounds with every controller that tries to
   144	exploit the difference.
   145	
   146	\paragraph{What the engine is doing when it is not decoding.} SGLang exposes
   147	two counters that answer this directly: tokens evicted from the radix cache,
   148	and prompt tokens that had to be prefilled rather than reused. Across the five
   149	static caps \emph{without} HiCache the two track each other almost exactly.
   150	\texttt{cap1} evicts 9.2\,M tokens and prefills 8.6\,M uncached;
   151	\texttt{cap5} evicts 21.3\,M and prefills 20.7\,M; the gap is 0.6--0.7\,M on
   152	every one of the five. An evicted prefix is recomputed, close to one token for
   153	one, and that recomputation is chunked into decode steps. Those same five runs
   154	give recomputation rising with $\tau$ across four of the five points: 9.2\,M
   155	uncached tokens at 27.4\,ms, 11.5 at 29.7, 15.4 at 32.4, 20.7 at 36.7. The
   156	exception is the first point, \texttt{cap1}, which recomputes least (8.6\,M)
   157	and still costs 28.8\,ms---more than \texttt{cap2} does for more recomputation.
   158	We report the ordering we have rather than rounding it to a monotone one: it is
   159	a mechanism consistent with the $\tau$ trend on four of the five device-only
   160	configurations, and one point it does not order. It is five points on five
   161	different policies, not an
   162	intervention: we did not hold concurrency fixed and vary eviction, so we call
   163	it consistent rather than demonstrated.
   164	
   165	\begin{figure}[tb]
   166	  \centering
   167	  \includegraphics[width=\columnwidth]{figs/fig7_elasticity.pdf}
   168	  \caption{Per-step cost against the running count during busy time. Solid
   169	  circles are $\tau_1$ and squares $\tau_2$ for the five device-only
   170	  configurations; $\tau_1$ is model-consistent (it averages over all engine
   171	  steps, including prefill that emits no output token), $\tau_2$ is the
   172	  per-request decode interval, and $\tau_1$ exceeds $\tau_2$ by 8--40\%. The
   173	  green triangles are $\tau_1$ for the three HiCache configurations---the ones
   174	  the controllers run---and they are drawn against the same axes to make
   175	  \S\ref{sec:criterion}'s point visible: over $\overline{R}_{\text{busy}}$ 1.25
   176	  to 1.73, which covers the whole controller operating range, the host-tier
   177	  curve rises 27.0 to 29.8\,ms while the device-only curve over a comparable
   178	  span rises 27.4 to 36.7. We plot $\tau_1$ only for the HiCache points to keep
   179	  the figure readable. The fitted exponent is the elasticity $\varepsilon$ of
   180	  Eq.~\ref{eq:elasticity}; it is fit to the device-only points alone.}
   181	  \label{fig:cancel}
   182	\end{figure}
   183	
   184	\paragraph{Where the mechanism stops.} With HiCache enabled the
   185	correspondence breaks---and it breaks in a way that confirms the mechanism
   186	rather than undermining it. The device cache misses as much as before:
   187	\texttt{cap4\_hc} misses 22.4\,M tokens on the device, more than the
   188	device-only \texttt{cap4} misses. But only 9.1\,M of that is recomputed; the
   189	other 13.3\,M is served from the host tier. Across the eight host-tier runs
   190	the host serves 13--19\,M tokens each, and across the five device-only runs it
   191	serves \emph{exactly zero}---the device miss equals the recomputed count to
   192	within rounding on all five. Recomputation is still what the device cannot
   193	absorb; the host tier is a second absorber in front of it.
   194	
   195	\subsection{The tier, not the policy, decides whether reuse survives}
   196	
   197	The device-miss column hides the quantity that matters. Define
   198	\emph{reuse} as the prompt tokens served from anywhere in the memory
   199	hierarchy---device radix cache plus host tier---so that
   200	$\text{reuse} + \text{recomputed} = \text{prompt}$, with the prompt fixed at
   201	28.5\,M by the trace.
   202	
   203	Reuse has an empirical ceiling in our data: \emph{no} configuration reuses
   204	more than 19.9\,M of the 28.5\,M, because that is all the overlap the session
   205	history contains (the trace's own mean planned hit rate is 0.738, and
   206	round-one requests have no prefix to reuse at all). The ceiling is a property
   207	of the workload. \textbf{Whether it is realized is a property of the memory
   208	tier}, and the two families diverge sharply:
   209	
   210	\begin{center}
   211	\begin{tabular}{lccccc}
   212	\toprule
   213	device-only & $\overline{R}_{\text{busy}}$ & 1.00 & 1.27 & 1.53 & 1.65 & 1.84 \\
   214	             & reuse (\%)                & 69.8 & 67.7 & 59.6 & 46.0 & 27.4 \\
   215	             & recomputed (M)            &  8.6 &  9.2 & 11.5 & 15.4 & 20.7 \\
   216	\midrule
   217	host tier    & $\overline{R}_{\text{busy}}$ & 1.25 & 1.51 & 1.73 & & \\
   218	             & reuse (\%)                & 69.8 & 68.8 & 68.1 & & \\
   219	             & recomputed (M)            &  8.6 &  8.9 &  9.1 & & \\
   220	\bottomrule
   221	\end{tabular}
   222	\end{center}
   223	
   224	On the device-only family reuse collapses from 69.8\% to 27.4\% and the
   225	shortfall appears one for one as recomputation, 8.6\,M to 20.7\,M. On the
   226	host-tier family the same ceiling is held across the whole range---69.8\% to
   227	68.1\% between $\overline{R}_{\text{busy}}$ 1.25 and 1.73---and it holds while
   228	the \emph{device} misses are larger than the device-only family's at every
   229	point (22.4--27.9\,M against 8.6--20.7\,M), because the host serves
   230	13--19\,M in front of the recomputation instead of letting the device keep it.
   231	A reader watching only the device hit rate would conclude the host tier made
   232	things worse; the reuse column says it is the only configuration that keeps
   233	the ceiling.
   234	
   235	This is the same collapse \S\ref{sec:related} reports under a different
   236	normalization, and the two agree: the round-1-and-later prefix hit rate---%
   237	which excludes round-zero steps, where there is no prefix to reuse---falls
   238	from 80.8\% to 32.8\% across the device-only caps and holds at 80.7\% to
   239	78.7\% with a host tier. The two conventions differ only in the denominator,
   240	so we use one here (all steps, token-weighted, so that reuse and
   241	recomputation add to the prompt) and name the other when we quote it.
   242	
   243	Device occupancy does not explain the split. \texttt{cap3\_fixed} and
   244	\texttt{cap3\_hc} run at nearly the same mean device occupancy (55.8\% and
   245	55.5\%) and nearly the same running count (1.53 and 1.51), and they realize
   246	59.6\% and 68.8\% of the ceiling respectively. What differs is not how full
   247	the device pool is but what happens when it overflows: with no host tier an
   248	evicted prefix is gone and must be recomputed; with one it is demoted and read
   249	back. The pool is the binding resource, the tier sets the pool, and the tier
   250	therefore sets whether added concurrency is paid for in recomputation.
   251	
   252	That gives the criterion of \S\ref{sec:criterion} a mechanism rather than a
   253	correlation, and it makes a falsifiable prediction: because the host tier
   254	multiplies the effective capacity (ratio 2 against the device pool), the
   255	reuse collapse should move to roughly three times the running count on the
   256	host-tier family---beyond any operating point our controllers reach, but
   257	within reach of a static cap. \S\ref{sec:results} reports the test.
   258	
   259	\paragraph{What is left unexplained.} Not everything here is accounted for.
   260	The host-tier runs sit at a flat 8.6--9.1\,M recomputed tokens \emph{and} a
   261	flat 68.1--69.8\% reuse, yet $\tau_1$ spans 27.0--35.7\,ms across them. Part of
   262	that is now explained and part is not. Three of those runs
   263	(\texttt{F:hz2} 33.6, \texttt{F:bres} 34.1, \texttt{F:hz0} 35.7\,ms) were
   264	measured while three other engine instances shared the host, which inflates
   265	per-step cost by 25--40\% with no effect on reuse at all
   266	(\S\ref{sec:repro}); they have been re-run. Excluding them the remaining five
   267	span 27.0--29.2\,ms at flat recomputation. That residual is small but it is
   268	not zero, and we do not attribute it. We note that the negative result does
   269	not rest on it: the cancellation is measured directly, not inferred from the
   270	mechanism.
   271	
   272	\paragraph{A refutation we had to withdraw.} An earlier revision withdrew this
   273	mechanism outright, on two checks that were both wrong. The first was that
   274	total prefilled tokens equalled the trace's planned prefill to a ratio of 1.00,
   275	so no token could have been computed twice. \texttt{prompt\_tokens\_total}
   276	counts the full submitted prompt \emph{including} its cached portion, so it
   277	equals the sum of prompt lengths by construction and cannot detect
   278	recomputation; the quantity that can is the uncached count used above, which
   279	varies by 2.4$\times$ across these runs. The second was that SGLang reported
   280	no retractions. Radix-cache eviction is not a retraction---retractions count
   281	running requests preempted mid-flight, a different event with its own counter.
   282	We record the episode because it is the same failure mode as the segment
   283	argument of \S\ref{sec:bg}: a check that looks like a measurement but is an
   284	identity.
   285	
   286	\subsection{Why idle time exists, and why recovering it does not pay}
   287	
   288	The length of an individual idle stretch is a property of the workload, not of
   289	the controller. A session in a
   290	tool wait holds its permit and its KV but issues no request; with only about
   291	two live sessions, the probability that all of them are simultaneously waiting
   292	is substantial. The direct evidence is the duration of the stretches
   293	themselves: they are of the same order as the tool waits, and an order of
   294	magnitude longer than the 2\,s control cycle (Fig.~\ref{fig:idle}). No
   295	controller can shorten a gap that the tool set.
   296	
   297	How much idle a \emph{run} accumulates is a different matter, and there the
   298	controller is not off the hook. Idle accumulates when every live session
   299	happens to be parked at once, and how many sessions are live is precisely what
   300	an admission policy sets. Two configurations with the same mean running count
   301	make this visible: \texttt{horizon}=1 (1.52) and \texttt{cap3}+HiCache (1.51)
   302	idle 36.7 and 24.1 minutes respectively---a 52\% difference at matched
   303	concurrency---and 12.6 of the 19.4-minute wall-clock gap between them is that
   304	idle difference rather than step cost. So the gap durations are the workload's,
   305	and the number of gaps is partly the policy's.
   306	
   307	We note one claim we had to drop here. An earlier draft reported that idle
   308	fraction falls monotonically with mean concurrency. With the full data it does
   309	not: \texttt{F:hz0} at 1.41 running requests idles 15.2\% while \texttt{cap4}
   310	at 1.48 idles 10.4\%. The weaker statement that survives is the one we use---%
   311	the stretches are structural and long.
   312	
   313	The obvious remedy---keep more sessions live so that someone is always
   314	ready---is what \texttt{bres} does. It works in the narrow sense: \texttt{bres}
   315	recovers about 17 of the minutes that its own baseline spends idle. It is
   316	still slower overall, because the sessions it keeps live raise the step cost
   317	by more than the recovered idle is worth. Idle is not unrecoverable; it is
   318	recoverable at a price that is not worth paying here.
   319	The structural remedy would be to make the engine do work during a wait, but
   320	there is no work to do: the session's next round does not exist until its tool
   321	returns.

   111	  circles are model-consistent (all engine steps), crosses are the per-request

   118	
   119	\subsection{The per-step cost is not constant in concurrency}
   120	
   121	Equation~\ref{eq:busy} would be uninteresting if $\tau$ were a constant. It is
   122	not. Across our 13 runs $\tau_1$ ranges from 27.0 to 36.7\,ms and $\tau_2$
   123	from 20.6 to 33.1\,ms, and both rise with the running count: fitting
   124	Eq.~\ref{eq:elasticity} to the five device-only configurations gives
   125	$\varepsilon = 0.38$ for $\tau_1$ and $0.61$ for $\tau_2$
   126	($R^2 = 0.89$ for the latter, Fig.~\ref{fig:cancel}).
   127	
   128	The relation is a trend, not a law. Two host-tier configurations with similar
   129	running counts behave differently: \texttt{cap4\_hc} runs 1.73 requests at
   130	29.8\,ms while \texttt{F:hz0} runs 1.66 at 35.7\,ms. Running count sets a
   131	floor and a trend, not a value---and on the host-tier family, as we show below,
   132	it does not set the value at all.
   133	
   134	This is the crux of the paper, and we state it at the strength the arithmetic
   135	supports. Hold $\tau_1$ at its value for \texttt{cap1}, 28.8\,ms, and busy
   136	time falls as $1/\overline{R}$: raising the running count from 1.00 to 1.84
   137	should have cut busy time by a factor of 1.84, from 340 minutes to 184. The
   138	measured curve flattens and lands at 234. The rise in $\tau$ takes back 50 of
   139	the 156 minutes that the extra concurrency would otherwise have bought---\emph{a
   140	third} of the gain, not all of it and, as an earlier revision of this paper
   141	claimed, not more than half. The other two thirds are real: raising
   142	concurrency does make the engine faster. What it does not do is make it faster
   143	in proportion, and the shortfall compounds with every controller that tries to
   144	exploit the difference.
   145	
   146	\paragraph{What the engine is doing when it is not decoding.} SGLang exposes
   147	two counters that answer this directly: tokens evicted from the radix cache,
   148	and prompt tokens that had to be prefilled rather than reused. Across the five
   149	static caps \emph{without} HiCache the two track each other almost exactly.
   150	\texttt{cap1} evicts 9.2\,M tokens and prefills 8.6\,M uncached;
   151	\texttt{cap5} evicts 21.3\,M and prefills 20.7\,M; the gap is 0.6--0.7\,M on
   152	every one of the five. An evicted prefix is recomputed, close to one token for
   153	one, and that recomputation is chunked into decode steps. Those same five runs
   154	give recomputation rising with $\tau$ across four of the five points: 9.2\,M
   155	uncached tokens at 27.4\,ms, 11.5 at 29.7, 15.4 at 32.4, 20.7 at 36.7. The
   156	exception is the first point, \texttt{cap1}, which recomputes least (8.6\,M)
   157	and still costs 28.8\,ms---more than \texttt{cap2} does for more recomputation.
   158	We report the ordering we have rather than rounding it to a monotone one: it is
   159	a mechanism consistent with the $\tau$ trend on four of the five device-only
   160	configurations, and one point it does not order. It is five points on five
   161	different policies, not an
   162	intervention: we did not hold concurrency fixed and vary eviction, so we call
   163	it consistent rather than demonstrated.
   164	
   165	\begin{figure}[tb]
   166	  \centering
   167	  \includegraphics[width=\columnwidth]{figs/fig7_elasticity.pdf}
   168	  \caption{Per-step cost against the running count during busy time. Solid
   169	  circles are $\tau_1$ and squares $\tau_2$ for the five device-only
   170	  configurations; $\tau_1$ is model-consistent (it averages over all engine
   171	  steps, including prefill that emits no output token), $\tau_2$ is the
   172	  per-request decode interval, and $\tau_1$ exceeds $\tau_2$ by 8--40\%. The
   173	  green triangles are $\tau_1$ for the three HiCache configurations---the ones
   174	  the controllers run---and they are drawn against the same axes to make
   175	  \S\ref{sec:criterion}'s point visible: over $\overline{R}_{\text{busy}}$ 1.25
   176	  to 1.73, which covers the whole controller operating range, the host-tier
   177	  curve rises 27.0 to 29.8\,ms while the device-only curve over a comparable
   178	  span rises 27.4 to 36.7. We plot $\tau_1$ only for the HiCache points to keep
   179	  the figure readable. The fitted exponent is the elasticity $\varepsilon$ of
   180	  Eq.~\ref{eq:elasticity}; it is fit to the device-only points alone.}
   181	  \label{fig:cancel}
   182	\end{figure}
   183	
   184	\paragraph{Where the mechanism stops.} With HiCache enabled the
   185	correspondence breaks---and it breaks in a way that confirms the mechanism
   186	rather than undermining it. The device cache misses as much as before:
   187	\texttt{cap4\_hc} misses 22.4\,M tokens on the device, more than the
   188	device-only \texttt{cap4} misses. But only 9.1\,M of that is recomputed; the
   189	other 13.3\,M is served from the host tier. Across the eight host-tier runs
   190	the host serves 13--19\,M tokens each, and across the five device-only runs it
   191	serves \emph{exactly zero}---the device miss equals the recomputed count to
   192	within rounding on all five. Recomputation is still what the device cannot
   193	absorb; the host tier is a second absorber in front of it.
   194	
   195	\subsection{The tier, not the policy, decides whether reuse survives}
   196	
   197	The device-miss column hides the quantity that matters. Define
   198	\emph{reuse} as the prompt tokens served from anywhere in the memory
   199	hierarchy---device radix cache plus host tier---so that
   200	$\text{reuse} + \text{recomputed} = \text{prompt}$, with the prompt fixed at
   201	28.5\,M by the trace.
   202	
   203	Reuse has an empirical ceiling in our data: \emph{no} configuration reuses
   204	more than 19.9\,M of the 28.5\,M, because that is all the overlap the session
   205	history contains (the trace's own mean planned hit rate is 0.738, and
   206	round-one requests have no prefix to reuse at all). The ceiling is a property
   207	of the workload. \textbf{Whether it is realized is a property of the memory
   208	tier}, and the two families diverge sharply:
   209	
   210	\begin{center}
   211	\begin{tabular}{lccccc}
   212	\toprule
   213	device-only & $\overline{R}_{\text{busy}}$ & 1.00 & 1.27 & 1.53 & 1.65 & 1.84 \\
   214	             & reuse (\%)                & 69.8 & 67.7 & 59.6 & 46.0 & 27.4 \\
   215	             & recomputed (M)            &  8.6 &  9.2 & 11.5 & 15.4 & 20.7 \\
   216	\midrule
   217	host tier    & $\overline{R}_{\text{busy}}$ & 1.25 & 1.51 & 1.73 & & \\
   218	             & reuse (\%)                & 69.8 & 68.8 & 68.1 & & \\
   219	             & recomputed (M)            &  8.6 &  8.9 &  9.1 & & \\
   220	\bottomrule
   221	\end{tabular}
   222	\end{center}
   223	
   224	On the device-only family reuse collapses from 69.8\% to 27.4\% and the
   225	shortfall appears one for one as recomputation, 8.6\,M to 20.7\,M. On the
   226	host-tier family the same ceiling is held across the whole range---69.8\% to
   227	68.1\% between $\overline{R}_{\text{busy}}$ 1.25 and 1.73---and it holds while
   228	the \emph{device} misses are larger than the device-only family's at every
   229	point (22.4--27.9\,M against 8.6--20.7\,M), because the host serves
   230	13--19\,M in front of the recomputation instead of letting the device keep it.
   231	A reader watching only the device hit rate would conclude the host tier made
   232	things worse; the reuse column says it is the only configuration that keeps
   233	the ceiling.
   234	
   235	This is the same collapse \S\ref{sec:related} reports under a different
   236	normalization, and the two agree: the round-1-and-later prefix hit rate---%
   237	which excludes round-zero steps, where there is no prefix to reuse---falls
   238	from 80.8\% to 32.8\% across the device-only caps and holds at 80.7\% to
   239	78.7\% with a host tier. The two conventions differ only in the denominator,
   240	so we use one here (all steps, token-weighted, so that reuse and
   241	recomputation add to the prompt) and name the other when we quote it.
   242	
   243	Device occupancy does not explain the split. \texttt{cap3\_fixed} and
   244	\texttt{cap3\_hc} run at nearly the same mean device occupancy (55.8\% and
   245	55.5\%) and nearly the same running count (1.53 and 1.51), and they realize
   246	59.6\% and 68.8\% of the ceiling respectively. What differs is not how full
   247	the device pool is but what happens when it overflows: with no host tier an
   248	evicted prefix is gone and must be recomputed; with one it is demoted and read
   249	back. The pool is the binding resource, the tier sets the pool, and the tier
   250	therefore sets whether added concurrency is paid for in recomputation.
   251	
   252	That gives the criterion of \S\ref{sec:criterion} a mechanism rather than a
   253	correlation, and it makes a falsifiable prediction: because the host tier
   254	multiplies the effective capacity (ratio 2 against the device pool), the
   255	reuse collapse should move to roughly three times the running count on the
   256	host-tier family---beyond any operating point our controllers reach, but
   257	within reach of a static cap. \S\ref{sec:results} reports the test.
   258	
   259	\paragraph{What is left unexplained.} Not everything here is accounted for.
   260	The host-tier runs sit at a flat 8.6--9.1\,M recomputed tokens \emph{and} a
   261	flat 68.1--69.8\% reuse, yet $\tau_1$ spans 27.0--35.7\,ms across them. Part of
   262	that is now explained and part is not. Three of those runs
   263	(\texttt{F:hz2} 33.6, \texttt{F:bres} 34.1, \texttt{F:hz0} 35.7\,ms) were
   264	measured while three other engine instances shared the host, which inflates
   265	per-step cost by 25--40\% with no effect on reuse at all
   266	(\S\ref{sec:repro}); they have been re-run. Excluding them the remaining five
   267	span 27.0--29.2\,ms at flat recomputation. That residual is small but it is
   268	not zero, and we do not attribute it. We note that the negative result does
   269	not rest on it: the cancellation is measured directly, not inferred from the
   270	mechanism.
   271	
   272	\paragraph{A refutation we had to withdraw.} An earlier revision withdrew this
   273	mechanism outright, on two checks that were both wrong. The first was that
   274	total prefilled tokens equalled the trace's planned prefill to a ratio of 1.00,
   275	so no token could have been computed twice. \texttt{prompt\_tokens\_total}
   276	counts the full submitted prompt \emph{including} its cached portion, so it
   277	equals the sum of prompt lengths by construction and cannot detect
   278	recomputation; the quantity that can is the uncached count used above, which
   279	varies by 2.4$\times$ across these runs. The second was that SGLang reported
   280	no retractions. Radix-cache eviction is not a retraction---retractions count
   281	running requests preempted mid-flight, a different event with its own counter.
   282	We record the episode because it is the same failure mode as the segment
   283	argument of \S\ref{sec:bg}: a check that looks like a measurement but is an
   284	identity.
   285	
   286	\subsection{Why idle time exists, and why recovering it does not pay}
   287	
   288	The length of an individual idle stretch is a property of the workload, not of
   289	the controller. A session in a
   290	tool wait holds its permit and its KV but issues no request; with only about
   291	two live sessions, the probability that all of them are simultaneously waiting
   292	is substantial. The direct evidence is the duration of the stretches
   293	themselves: they are of the same order as the tool waits, and an order of
   294	magnitude longer than the 2\,s control cycle (Fig.~\ref{fig:idle}). No
   295	controller can shorten a gap that the tool set.
   296	
   297	How much idle a \emph{run} accumulates is a different matter, and there the
   298	controller is not off the hook. Idle accumulates when every live session
   299	happens to be parked at once, and how many sessions are live is precisely what
   300	an admission policy sets. Two configurations with the same mean running count
   301	make this visible: \texttt{horizon}=1 (1.52) and \texttt{cap3}+HiCache (1.51)
   302	idle 36.7 and 24.1 minutes respectively---a 52\% difference at matched
   303	concurrency---and 12.6 of the 19.4-minute wall-clock gap between them is that
   304	idle difference rather than step cost. So the gap durations are the workload's,
   305	and the number of gaps is partly the policy's.
   306	
   307	We note one claim we had to drop here. An earlier draft reported that idle
   308	fraction falls monotonically with mean concurrency. With the full data it does
   309	not: \texttt{F:hz0} at 1.41 running requests idles 15.2\% while \texttt{cap4}
   310	at 1.48 idles 10.4\%. The weaker statement that survives is the one we use---%
   311	the stretches are structural and long.
   312	
   313	The obvious remedy---keep more sessions live so that someone is always
   314	ready---is what \texttt{bres} does. It works in the narrow sense: \texttt{bres}
   315	recovers about 17 of the minutes that its own baseline spends idle. It is

     1	\section{A Criterion for When Admission Control Can Pay}
     2	\label{sec:criterion}
     3	
     4	The decomposition turns the
     5	question into one number. Throughput during busy time is
     6	$\overline{R}/\tau$, so raising concurrency by a factor $f$ buys a factor
     7	$f^{1-\epsilon}$, where
     8	\begin{equation}
     9	  \epsilon \;=\; \frac{\mathrm{d}\ln \tau}{\mathrm{d}\ln \overline{R}}
    10	  \label{eq:elasticity}
    11	\end{equation}
    12	is the elasticity of per-step cost with respect to the running count. At
    13	$\epsilon = 0$ the step cost is flat, concurrency converts one for one into
    14	throughput, and an admission controller has room to work. At $\epsilon = 1$
    15	the two cancel exactly and it has none. Everything in between is a
    16	quantitative statement about how much room.
    17	
    18	On the five device-only configurations, where \S\ref{sec:model} establishes
    19	that the step cost is driven by recomputation, $\overline{R}_{\text{busy}}$
    20	runs from 1.00 to 1.84. A least-squares fit of Eq.~\ref{eq:elasticity} over
    21	those five points gives $\epsilon = 0.61$ on the per-request measurement
    22	($R^2 = 0.89$), or $0.38$ on the model-consistent one (Fig.~\ref{fig:cancel}).
    23	
    24	That fit is a straight line through a curve, and the curve is what matters. The
    25	segment elasticities are $\epsilon = -0.21$ from \texttt{cap1} to
    26	\texttt{cap2}, $+0.43$ from \texttt{cap2} to \texttt{cap3}, $+1.15$ from
    27	\texttt{cap3} to \texttt{cap4}, and $+1.14$ from \texttt{cap4} to
    28	\texttt{cap5}. Below $\overline{R}_{\text{busy}} \approx 1.5$ added
    29	concurrency still buys something; above it, $\epsilon$ passes one, which means
    30	the engine gets \emph{slower} in wall clock as more requests run. The single
    31	number $0.38$ averages those regimes and describes none of them.
    32	
    33	The operating region is the one that matters, and it is not the region the
    34	controllers run in. All nine controller arms run with HiCache enabled, which
    35	the five device-only caps above do not. Measuring the same elasticity on the
    36	three HiCache configurations we have gives $\epsilon = +0.19$ from
    37	\texttt{cap2}+HC to \texttt{cap3}+HC and $+0.46$ from \texttt{cap3}+HC to
    38	\texttt{cap4}+HC, over $\overline{R}_{\text{busy}}$ 1.25 to 1.73. That range
    39	covers the whole controller operating region (1.35 to 1.69), and over it
    40	$\epsilon$ never approaches one. At matched running counts the HiCache caps pay
    41	$1.7$\,ms less per step at $\overline{R}_{\text{busy}} \approx 1.5$.
    42	
    43	This contradicts what an earlier draft of this paper claimed, and it changes
    44	what the criterion is for. The cliff in the device-only curve is a
    45	recomputation effect: \S\ref{sec:model} measures that those runs recompute what
    46	they evict, while under HiCache the host tier absorbs it. The cliff does not
    47	survive on the configuration our controllers actually use. So the criterion
    48	does \emph{not} explain why they lose---on their own configuration they were
    49	not in a region where added concurrency was self-defeating, and the engine had
    50	room they did not take. What the measurement does is rule one explanation out.
    51	The failure is not that this workload denies admission control its lever; it is
    52	that our controllers do not pull the lever they have, which is what
    53	\S\ref{sec:results:idle-memory} and the attribution of
    54	\S\ref{sec:results:attribution} measure directly.
    55	
    56	We are explicit about what this does and does not establish. The HiCache curve
    57	rests on three configurations and two segments, one run each, with no
    58	error bars, and it stops at 1.73---we cannot see whether a cliff appears
    59	beyond the controllers' region. A $\pm10\%$ perturbation of $\tau$ puts the
    60	segment elasticities' bounds across one even on the device-only curve, so the
    61	cliff is a description of the fitted points rather than a threshold we have
    62	located precisely. And it is measured on
    63	different policies rather than by an intervention, so it is a
    64	cross-policy association. On our data it is also computed from the same wall
    65	clock the model then explains---$\tau_1$ is derived from $W_{\text{busy}}$, so
    66	using it to predict $W_{\text{busy}}$ restates rather than tests---and its
    67	points are different policies, so the slope is a cross-policy association, not
    68	a transportable causal derivative. $\tau_2$ is genuinely independent of the
    69	wall clock but measures only decode, so it cannot be dropped into a throughput
    70	equation that includes prefill.
    71	
    72	We tested whether the curve transfers to a second workload and it does not
    73	obviously do so. On a real agent trace (117 sessions, tool waits of seconds
    74	rather than the 95\,ms of the synthetic trace, three static caps and one
    75	controller arm), the model-consistent elasticity is negative across the measured
    76	range---$-2.40$ then $-0.28$, at $\overline{R}_{\text{busy}}$ 1.08 to 1.29---and
    77	the controller arm there runs at 1.22, below every one of the nine arms above,
    78	and still does not beat the static front. The per-request measure has the
    79	opposite sign on that trace ($+0.41$, $+0.48$). We report this as a boundary:
    80	the criterion is a description of one workload on one configuration, and where
    81	we could check it elsewhere it neither transferred nor explained the failure.
    82	It is not, as an earlier draft claimed, something that decides the question in
    83	minutes before building anything.

    79	"会被杀"，**这次发现的是更早的代价：即使没被杀，数据也已经不可比了**。
    80	
    81	## 审计结果
    82	
    83	`paper/data/concurrency.csv`（123 条 run）：
    84	
    85	- **CONTESTED（同时 ≥4 条持续 ≥60 分钟）12 条**
    86	- watch（≥3 条持续 ≥60 分钟）4 条
    87	- clean 107 条
    88	
    89	表 I 里受影响的臂正好 4 条，全部是控制器变体：
    90	
    91	| run | 峰值 | ≥4 条持续 | τ₂ |
    92	|---|---|---|---|
    93	| `foyer_breshz1` | 4 | 296 分钟 | 33.3 |
    94	| `foyer_hz0` | 4 | 296 分钟 | 33.1 |
    95	| `foyer_bres` | 4 | 296 分钟 | 31.3 |
    96	| `foyer_hz2` | 4 | 282 分钟 | 30.9 |
    97	
    98	其余 CONTESTED 的 8 条都不在表 I：rl90 / rl99 / t85 / t90（rl 与 t 的扫描）、
    99	var_foyer / var_cap3（变负载族）、以及 09-24 被 SIGTERM 的两条 `_failed_4lane_*`。
   100	
   101	`pois200_cap1` 只有 28.8 分钟处于 ≥4 条，记 watch；它的 τ₂ 20.6 是全场最低，
   102	未受影响。
   103	
   104	## 影响估计（重测之前，只作量级参考，不进正文）
   105	
   106	τ 若按同族干净水平折算，这四条的忙时会缩短约 22%，墙钟大致：
   107	
   108	```
   109	foyer_bres    295.5 → ~247 分    对前沿在该点的插值 91.6  →  Δ 由 −7.8 变到约 −0.6
   110	```
   111	
   112	**如果这个量级成立，表 I 的判决会变**——这四条的 Δ 可能从"明显在线下"变成
   113	"贴着线"。所以必须重测，不能靠折算。
   114	
   115	## 已采取的动作
   116	
   117	1. **重跑这四条**（`lane_foyer_r2_batch1.sh` / `lane_foyer_r2_batch2.sh`），
   118	   新 run 名加 `_r2` 后缀，严格 2 条车道。旧目录原样保留，不覆盖不删除。
   119	2. 重跑用 `wait_and_launch.sh` 排队：等前一条车道的**最后一个 run 写出 summary**
   120	   （不是"显存空"——run_matrix 在一条车道的两条 run 之间会卸载 server，
   121	   那一刻显存是空的但车道没结束），启动前再数一次自己的 server 数。
   122	3. 审计脚本进仓，`paper/data/concurrency.csv` 由它生成。
   123	
   124	## 对论文的意义（要如实写）
   125	
   126	- **表 I 的这四行在重测前不可信**。重测完用新 run 替换，并在可复现性一节
   127	  说明为什么换、怎么发现的。
   128	- 这是一条**方法论发现**，本身值得写：内存受限 serving 的测量对同机并发
   129	  **高度敏感**，而敏感性只体现在 `τ₂` 这种**逐请求的客户端量**上——引擎侧
   130	  `num_running_reqs`、占用率、驱逐量、重算量**全都看不出异常**（这四条臂在这四列上
   131	  与干净 run 不可区分）。如果只盯着引擎指标，这批数据会被当成好的。
   132	- 与 §III 那条"最大未解问题"的关系：论文原来写"HiCache 族重算恒定而 τ 散开，
   133	  我们不知道为什么"。**其中一部分现在有答案了——那四条臂的 τ 不是被什么都西推动的，
   134	  是被同机争用推动的**。剩下五条（hc/hz1/hz2 之外的）重算恒定而 τ 在 27.0~29.2 之间
   135	  小范围移动，这是重测之后要重新面对的问题，范围比原来小得多。
     1	# 发现：判据曲线在控制器臂的实际配置下没有悬崖
     2	
     3	日期：2026-09-23
     4	脚本：`scripts/lab/gen_elasticity_hc.py` → `paper/data/elasticity_hc.csv`
     5	起因：codex 审稿意见 #1 —— 「分段弹性来自**无 HiCache 的五个静态 cap**，
     6	而九条控制器臂**全部开着 HiCache**；论文自己承认 HiCache 下相近运行数可对应
     7	明显不同的步成本。」
     8	
     9	## 两条曲线（同一个 tau 口径：busy 条件均值，见 gen_arm_running.py）
    10	
    11	```
    12	device-only（论文 §IV 现在唯一用的那条）
    13	  cap1         Rbar 1.00  tau 28.8
    14	  cap2         Rbar 1.27  tau 27.4     eps = -0.208
    15	  cap3_fixed   Rbar 1.53  tau 29.7     eps = +0.433
    16	  cap4         Rbar 1.65  tau 32.4     eps = +1.152   <-- 悬崖
    17	  cap5         Rbar 1.84  tau 36.7     eps = +1.143   <-- 悬崖
    18	
    19	HiCache（九条控制器臂实际运行的配置）
    20	  pairB_cap2hc Rbar 1.25  tau 27.0
    21	  cap3_hc      Rbar 1.51  tau 28.0     eps = +0.192
    22	  cap4_hc      Rbar 1.73  tau 29.8     eps = +0.458
    23	```
    24	
    25	**HiCache 曲线在控制器的整个运行区间（Rbar 1.25–1.73）内 eps 从未达到 1。**
    26	device-only 曲线有 2/4 段 eps>=1，HiCache 曲线 0/2 段。
    27	
    28	run 身份已核实：`pois200_pairB_cap2hc` / `pois200_cap3_hc` / `pois200_cap4_hc`，
    29	policy 分别为 static=2/3/4，pool 全部 101432（可比）。控制器车道脚本
    30	（`lane_var_foyer_g2.sh` 等）均设 `TURNSTILE_HICACHE_ARGS`，即控制器确实开 HiCache。
    31	
    32	## 后果一：§IV 现在的解释对控制器不成立
    33	
    34	论文现写：「Five sit at 1.63 or above, in the eps ≈ 1.15 segment where it
    35	does not pay at all.」—— 这句话把 device-only 的曲线套到 HiCache 的臂上。
    36	按控制器自己的配置，它们所在的段 eps 只有 0.19–0.46，**加并发仍然划算**。
    37	
    38	物理上也讲得通：device-only 的悬崖来自**重算**（§III 已测：五条 device-only run
    39	的 device miss 恰好等于重算 token，host 吸收为 0），而 HiCache 把驱逐的 KV 落到
    40	host 再读回，替代了重算，悬崖因此消失。τ 在同 Rbar 下的对比也支持：
    41	Rbar≈1.7 处 device tau=32.4–36.7，HiCache tau=29.8。
    42	
    43	## 后果二：19 分钟的那个反例，其实是 idle 不是步成本
    44	
    45	论文 sec_results.tex:156 用「hz1 与 cap3_hc 占用相同、Rbar 几乎相同，cap 却快 19 分钟」
    46	论证「低占用不能解释结果」，并把差额归给步成本。拆开 decom：
    47	
    48	```
    49	              Rbar_busy  idle_min  busy_min   wall_min
    50	cap3_hc         1.51       24.1      218.4     236.4
    51	foyer_hz1       1.52       36.7      225.2     255.8
    52	差                          +12.6      +6.8     +19.4
    53	```
    54	
    55	**65% 的差额（12.6/19.4）是 idle 时间**，不是步成本。两者步成本只差
    56	1.2 ms（29.2 vs 28.0，约 4%）。idle 变多与「控制器攥着 permit 不放」一致：
    57	会话一轮结束去 tool_wait 时，控制器不放进新的，引擎就空转。
    58	
    59	## 这对论文意味着什么
    60	
    61	现在稿子里有两条互相竞争的解释：
    62	  (a) 步成本悬崖（eps>=1）让加并发不划算
    63	  (b) 控制器高估占用 1.79 倍、只用到 49.2% 池子，不敢放
    64	
    65	新数据说 **(a) 在控制器实际配置下不存在**，所以 (b) 才是主因。
    66	这实际上是个**更好**的故事：判据的作用从「解释失败」变成「**排除掉工作负载侧的
    67	解释**，从而把失败定位在控制器自己身上」——这正是一个诊断工具该干的事，
    68	而且它不再需要跨配置外推。
    69	
    70	代价：摘要里「that is a property of the workload rather than of the controller」
    71	一句需要重写，§IV 的「operating region」一节需要重写。
    72	
    73	## 尚未确定 / 需要补的
    74	
    75	- HiCache 曲线只到 Rbar 1.73（没有 cap5_hc），**看不见更高处有没有悬崖**。
    76	  控制器区间（1.35–1.71）落在已测范围内，结论对该区间成立；对更高 Rbar 未知。
    77	- 每条 HiCache 静态 cap 只有一条 run（cap3_hc/cap4_hc 各有一条重复在跑/已有），
    78	  段弹性没有误差棒。
    79	- 控制器是否真的因 idle 而慢，需要在**全部**臂上验证 idle_pct 与 wall 的关系，
    80	  不能只看 hz1/cap3_hc 这一对。

    83	
    84	# 追加发现（同日）：变负载 trace 上控制器投影饱和
    85	
    86	在 `pois200_var_foyer`（控制器主臂 `budget3:hw=0;target=0.95`，跑变负载 trace
    87	`replay_varload_lo2hi.csv`）上观测到准入停滞。核实过程：
    88	
    89	```
    90	waiting_n   = 22 ~ 23     有会话在等
    91	resident    = 0           引擎占用的 token 数（设备侧）
    92	usage       = 0.0
    93	valve       = False        SLO 阀门【没有】关
    94	gate_full   = False        round gate 没开（默认 round_gate=0）
    95	candidate   = None
    96	n_admitted  = 0
    97	```
    98	
    99	引擎侧同时是空的：metrics.csv 里 `num_running_reqs=0`、`num_used_tokens=0`、
   100	`num_queue_reqs=0`，`generation_tokens_total` 冻结在 136501。
   101	
   102	## 根因
   103	
   104	`controller_budget3.py:321-326`：
   105	
   106	```
   107	ctx_sum    = 72100     # 【在跑的 2 个会话】的上下文之和，各约 36000 token
   108	growth_sum = 26737
   109	base       = max(resident, ctx_sum) = 72100
   110	projection = base + growth_sum = 98837
   111	budget     = 96360
   112	```
   113	
   114	判定是 `if projection + n <= budget`（:406）。**projection 本身已经超过 budget，
   115	所以对任何候选者都不成立**，`candidates` 恒为空 → 一个都不放行。
   116	
   117	`ctx_sum` 中位 47164、最大 123312；`base` 最大 103469，均可达/超过 budget 96360。
   118	
   119	唯一在推进的是 starve guard（:413-419），它 bypass 预算直接强推 `waiting[0]`，
   120	但有 `--starve-cooldown`（默认 300 秒）限速。实测 admit 间隔中位 87 秒，
   121	最后几次 199 / 278 / 343 / 302 秒 —— 就是 cooldown 的节拍。70 分钟里放了 35 个。
   122	
   123	## 为什么这不是 bug，而是论文机制的极端形态
   124	
   125	投影把**停在 tool_wait 里的会话按完整上下文计费**，而它们的 KV 是可驱逐的、
   126	并不在引擎的 resident 计数里。这正是 §V「控制器认为快满了，其实空着一半」
   127	（投影 1.79 倍于真值）描述的同一个量，只是在这条 trace 上被推到了极端：
   128	**只要有两个大上下文会话同时停在 tool wait，控制器就完全停止接纳，而引擎空转。**
   129	
   130	论文 §V 的 Route 1 已经测过：把这个 double charge 去掉（`base=resident`）反而
   131	更差（慢 4.2 分钟、SLO −6.0pp）。所以控制器是按设计行事。
   132	
   133	## 对变负载实验的影响（待定，取决于最终能否跑完）
   134	
   135	- 按当前速率，200 个会话约需 6.7 小时，可能落在 12h 超时内。
   136	- 若跑完：这是一个**可用但极端**的数据点 —— 控制器在变负载下不是"输一点"，
   137	  而是退化成每 5 分钟放一个。
   138	- 若跑不完：按项目规矩记为部分 run，不与 999 步的臂同表比较。
   139	- **需要在论文里如实报告这个机制**，否则审稿人会把它当成隐藏的 bug。
   140	
   141	---
   142	
   143	# 追加发现（09-24 凌晨）：变负载切换基线第一次跑无效，根因是仪表坏了
   144	
   145	## 现象
   146	
   147	`pois200_var_switch` 跑完（999 步、250.9 分钟、rc=0），但 `switcher.jsonl` 只有
   148	**1 条**记录（t=0，cap=3），而 `--interval 2` 下 250 分钟本该写约 7500 条。
   149	
   150	## 排除
   151	
   152	`cap_switcher.py` 的日志是**条件写入**的：
   153	
   154	```python
   155	if nxt != cap or wrote is None:   # 只在 cap 变化时记
   156	```
   157	
   158	所以「只有 1 条」不等于「死了」，而是**一直在跑但从未切换**。
   159	
   160	## 根因
   161	
   162	```python
   163	EVENTS = ("queued",)     # 只数 queued 事件当到达
   164	```
   165	
   166	但 `static=N` 模式下 runner 立即放行、没有东西压着会话，
   167	`admissions.jsonl` 里 **400 条全是 admit 和 release，一个 queued 都没有**。
   168	于是 `n_recent` 恒为 0、`rate` 恒为 0、`hi_streak` 永远到不了 2，**全程一次没切**。
   169	
   170	**所以第一次的 `pois200_var_switch` 实际等价于一条静态 cap3 run**（cap 文件被写成
   171	3 后再没动过），仍可当 cap3 的数据点用，不必浪费。
   172	
   173	## 修法（改仪表，不改判据）
   174	
   175	第一次尝试改成数 `admit` 事件 —— **错了**，离线模拟显示它在 82 分钟就切、89 分钟
   176	又切回来，之后再不切。原因是 `admit` 衡量的是**放行速率**，被 cap=3 限住了，那是
   177	容量节流不是到达。
   178	
   179	最终改法：用 `admit` 事件的 `waited_ms` **反推真实到达时刻**
   180	（`arrival = ts - waited_ms/1000`）。这是可部署的量（一个会话等了多久），
   181	不是偷看 trace。验证：
   182	
   183	```
   184	反推到达的速率： 0-125 分 0.009~0.019/s  ← 低载相
   185	              125-175 分 0.030~0.037/s  ← 高载相
   186	切点 125 分，与 trace 设计的 124.5 分吻合。
   187	```
   188	
   189	离线重放（拿第一次 run 的 admissions.jsonl 走修好的逻辑）：**t=128.0 分切到 cap2**，
   190	滞后 3.5 分钟；之后保持 cap2。
   191	
   192	**切换规则本身（hi 0.025 / lo 0.020 / 窗 600s / 连续两个采样）一个字未改。**
   193	
   194	## 顺带纠正一处我对规则的误读
   195	
   196	`hi_streak >= 2` 数的是**连续两个 2 秒采样**（4 秒去抖），不是「两个 10 分钟窗」；
   197	600 秒窗只提供平滑。所以 codex 担心的「要 20 分钟才降档」，实测滞后 3.5 分钟。
   198	
   199	## 另一个观察：cap 在变负载 trace 上并不顶满引擎
   200	
   201	`pois200_var_switch`（cap=3）全程 `sglang:num_running_reqs` 中位 1~2、
   202	`sglang:num_queue_reqs` 中位 0（最大 2）—— **cap 从未被在跑数顶满**。
   203	cap 限的是**活跃会话数**（含停在 tool_wait 的），不是在跑数，
   204	所以引擎空着而会话在排队（积压最高 67 个）。这与子 agent 在 realhr 上的发现一致。
   205	
   206	---
   207	
   208	# 追加（09-24 03:00）：四条车道把卡 0/1 的两条 run 跑废了
   209	
   210	## 事实
   211	
   212	02:36 起，卡 0 的 `pois200_foyer_sf0` 与卡 1 的 `pois200_foyer_hc_r2` 双双失败：
   213	
   214	```
   215	                 尝试    成功    失败
   216	foyer_hc_r2       624     528     96     ← 未跑满 999 步
   217	foyer_sf0         679     595     84     ← 未跑满 999 步
   218	```
   219	
   220	失败模式：`steps.jsonl` 里 `status=FAILED`、错误为
   221	`request error: ... connection closed before message completed`，
   222	从 148.4 分钟起成簇出现，成功请求在 148.8 分钟处**全部停止**。
   223	server 日志末尾：`Subprocess scheduler_0 (pid=...) crashed with exit code -15`。
   224	
   225	两条 run 的引擎死在**同一绝对时刻**（02:36:46 与 02:37:00，相隔 14 秒），
   226	所以是外部杀死，不是自己崩的。
   227	
   228	## 根因：我违反了项目已经记下的教训
   229	
   230	`scripts/watchdog.sh` 的注释里写着（2026-09-21 学到）：
   231	
   232	> CARDS 0 AND 1 ARE DELIBERATELY ABSENT. ... because **saturating all four gets
   233	> our long-running processes reaped by other tenants on this shared box.**
   234	
   235	我把车道从 2 条加到 4 条，于是长跑进程被共享机器上其它租户的 reaper 杀掉。
   236	**SIGTERM 是结果不是原因** —— 我去查谁发的 SIGTERM 是白费功夫。
   237	
   238	## 处置
   239	
   240	1. 撤掉卡 0/1 的 stream 与 gate；总并发回到 <= 2。
   241	2. 三条失败的 run 目录挪到 `_failed_4lane_*`（按项目规矩，同名目录必须先挪走，
   242	   否则追加式日志会把两次 run 静默混合）。
   243	3. 重新串行排队：卡 2 跑 var_cap2 → foyer_hc_r2 → var_switch2；
   244	   卡 3 跑 var_cap3 → foyer_sf0_r2。lane 脚本的卡号/端口已相应改掉。
   245	4. 教训写进 `CLAUDE.md`「硬件与运维」节。
   246	
   247	## 一条仍然有效的观察
   248	
   249	`var_foyer`（变负载控制器臂）在四条车道期间**跑完了 999 步、0 失败**，
   250	所以它没有受影响。四条变负载臂里已完成的是 var_foyer 与 var_switch（后者等价于
   251	静态 cap3）。

     9	- **论文里出现的每个数字都要能追到**代码、日志或 `paper/data/*.csv`。追不到的就标出来，
    10	  不要留在正文里。
    11	- **改 LaTeX 不许改科学含义**。措辞归措辞，结论归结论，两者分开说。
    12	- 下结论前先看文件。涉及实验的陈述必须来自 `results/`、`paper/data/` 或实验机日志。
    13	
    14	## 这个项目的三条血的教训
    15	
    16	1. **手算的表一定会错。** `paper/data/decomp.csv` 曾有一行 busy/idle 来自另一条 run、
    17	   wall 却是对的，两个数自相矛盾，图 2 因此画错，几个月没人发现。任何汇总表都应由
    18	   `scripts/lab/*.py` 生成并带断言（见 `gen_decomp.py`：busy+idle 必须精确等于采样跨度，
    19	   残差非零直接退出）。
    20	1b. **从错的列抄数 —— 已经犯过四次。** 手抄汇总表时把相邻一列的数当成目标列：
    21	   `pairB_cap2hc` 的 busy/idle 来自另一条 run、`foyer_hz2`/`foyer_hz0` 的 running
    22	   抄错、表 I 的 rg4/foyfix 两行 Δ 用了中途读数、相关工作的「round-1+ 命中率
    23	   89.8%」其实是 foyer_hc 的 **SLO**（真值 80.9%）。
    24	   **凡是从表里抄一个数，抄完回头看一眼它在原表里是哪一列。**
    25	
    26	2. **中位数会骗人。** `tool_wait` 中位 0.35 s 让整个项目以为会话不离开引擎；均值是 16.85 s。
    27	   报分布的时候，中位、均值、p90、最大值一起报。
    28	3. **"还没到" 和 "坏了" 长得一样。** 曾三次把「trace 的第一个会话还没到」误判成「实验卡住」。
    29	   判断一个 run 是否正常，先看 `runner.out` 和它已跑时长，别只看 steps=0。
    30	
    31	## 撤回记录
    32	
    33	已经撤回的主张不许复活，除非有新证据：`tool_wait` 0.1s→16.85s、oracle 单调性、
    34	记账重复计费、驱逐→重算推高步时间（被 `num_retracted_reqs≈0` 与
    35	`prompt_tokens_total == 计划 prefill` 反驳）、空转率随并发单调下降。
    36	详见 `paper/claims_audit.md` 与 `docs/claim_register_20260921.md`。

    38	## 引用红线
    39	
    40	- arXiv **2608.30830** 自报对 MORI/Continuum 的 head-to-head 胜利**已被否证，禁引**；
    41	  它的 41% goodput 损失与 ~10× 复活差可以引。
    42	- **PrefixShield (2608.01657)** 已占 "admission responsibility" 术语，需避撞。
    43	- 任何引用的标题、作者、年份都必须核实过才能进 `refs.bib`。**不要凭记忆写文献。**
    44	
    45	## 硬件与运维
    46	
    47	- 平时只用**卡 2 和卡 3**。卡 0/1 仅限 00:00–08:00 且确认空闲（`memory.used < 200 MiB`）。
    48	- **别人占着卡就等，不要算剩余空间够不够。只在完整的卡上跑。**
    49	- **不要同时占满四张卡。** 卡 0/1 可用时，总并发仍应 <= 2 条车道。
    50	  2026-09-21 学到过一次（记在 `scripts/watchdog.sh` 注释里）：把四张卡全占满，
    51	  长跑进程会被共享机器上其它租户的 reaper 杀掉。**2026-09-24 又犯了一次**：
    52	  四条车道一起开，卡 0/1 的两条 run 在 02:36:46 / 02:37:00（相隔 14 秒，
    53	  同一绝对时刻）双双被 SIGTERM，只跑到 679 和 624 步就终止，两条都作废。
    54	  症状：`steps.jsonl` 里 `connection closed before message completed` 在某个时刻
    55	  突然成簇出现，`status=FAILED`；server 日志里 `Subprocess scheduler_0 crashed
    56	  with exit code -15`。**这个 SIGTERM 是结果不是原因，别去查谁发的。**
    57	- **2026-09-24 追加：四条车道就算没被杀，数据也已经废了。** 审计（`scripts/lab/gen_concurrency.py`
    58	  → `paper/data/concurrency.csv`）查明：同机同时 ≥4 条 run 且持续 ≥60 分钟，
    59	  会把**逐请求 decode 间隔中位 τ₂ 抬高 25~40%**（30.9~33.3 ms vs 同族干净值 23.8~25.7）。
    60	  2~3 条不抬（pairB_foyerhc 3 条跑 277 分钟，τ₂=23.8，正常）。
    61	  **危险之处：引擎侧所有指标都看不出异常** —— `num_running_reqs`、占用率、驱逐量、
    62	  重算量在这四条臂上与干净 run 不可区分，只有客户端实测的 τ₂ 露馅。
    63	  四条控制器臂（bres/hz0/hz2/breshz1）因此作废并重跑（新名加 `_r2`）。
    64	  详见 `docs/finding_concurrency_contamination_20260924.md`。
    65	  **判据要按时长不按峰值**：`foyer_hz1` 峰值到过 4 条但只有 14 分钟，τ₂ 正常。
    66	- **跑实验时必须挂 `gpu_watch.py`**（2026-09-24 用户提出，已接入 `run_matrix.py`）：
    67	  池子守卫只管【启动那一刻】，跑到一半别人往同一张卡上挂进程会静默污染这一段的
    68	  tau / SLO / 墙钟，而数据看上去完全正常。watcher 每 15 秒记一次该卡的显存、
    69	  利用率、全部计算进程及其属主，输出到 `<run_dir>/gpu_watch.jsonl`；属主不是自己的
    70	  标成 foreign。汇总用 `gpu_watch_summary.py <rundir>`，有它就可以写"这次测量期间
    71	  该卡独占"。**这是低概率情况的便宜保险，不是对已有结果的质疑**：起跑时的池子守卫
    72	  已保证池子严格对齐，且多次重复之间墙钟 CV 只有 0.9~1.8% —— 真被偷算力不会这么稳。
    73	  所以早期 run 照常可信，不因缺这个记录而作废。
    74	- KV pool 必须落在 `101,432 ± 20`，否则该 run 作废，不记录——SGLang 按启动时的空闲显存
    75	  定池子，池子小了数字就不可比。
    76	
    77	## 改文件
    78	
    79	- **`open(path,"w")` 会在 write 之前就截断文件。** 2026-09-24 犯过：`s.replace(...)`
    80	  后面多打一个逗号使返回值成了元组，write 抛 TypeError，但文件已经被清空
    81	  （`sec_discussion.tex` 整篇丢失，页数从 15 掉到 12 是这个信号）。
    82	  规矩：写完先断言 `isinstance(s, str)`，或者先写临时文件再 `os.replace`。
    83	  征兆是**页数突然变少** —— 编译后页数掉了，先怀疑有文件被清空。
    84	- 长文档改完后 `git diff --stat` 看一眼增删行数，与预期差太多就停下查。
    85	
    86	## 语言
    87	
    88	- 对用户回复一律用**中文**。代码、路径、报错原文可保留英文。
    89	- TUI 里不要写 LaTeX 公式，用纯文本或中文名。
    90	
    91	## git
    92	
    93	commit message 里含反引号时**不要用 `-m`**，走 `-F 文件`——已因此静默丢词三次。
     1	# 主张 → 证据 审计（2026-09-23）
     2	
     3	> 目的：写正文之前，把活下来的每一条主张拉出来逐条查。
     4	> 判据是四样，**缺任何一样就降级或删掉**：
     5	>
     6	> 1. **实测数字**（不是推论）
     7	> 2. **对照**（不是孤立观测）
     8	> 3. **反例检查**（我们自己试过推翻它没有）
     9	> 4. **可复现路径**（命令 / 脚本 / run 名）
    10	>
    11	> 这个项目的**五条**撤回（`tool_wait` 0.1s→16.85s、oracle 单调性、重复计费、空转率单调性、以及「驱逐→重算」那次错误的反驳）都是因为缺了第 3 条。
    12	
    13	---
    14	
    15	## C1. 墙钟可以拆成「有效工作 + 空转」两部分
    16	
    17	**数字**（2026-09-23 修正为采样跨度口径）：`cap4_hc 202.8 + 16.1 = 218.9`（采样跨度，墙钟 213.0）；`foyer_hc 250.1 + 47.4 = 297.5`（墙钟 291.3）；`cap1 340.0 + 193.6 = 533.6`（墙钟 527.4）。各 run 的跨度比墙钟长约 6 分钟，是采样器早于第一个请求启动造成的。
    18	**对照**：11 条静态 + 9 条 Foyer，全表一致
    19	**反例检查**：`bres` 是反例形态的反例——它空转压到 10.1% 但有效工作涨到 271 分钟，**证明两项独立**
    20	**路径**：`scripts/frontier.py`；空转从 `metrics.csv` 的 `sglang:num_running_reqs == 0` 段累加
    21	
    22	**状态：可用。** 注意措辞——这是**分解**，不是"模型"。
    23	
    24	---
    25	
    26	## C2. 步时间可以被两个独立方法测到，且两者排序一致
    27	
    28	**数字**（反推 / 逐请求）：
    29	
    30	```
    31	cap4_hc   29.0 / 25.7 ms        cap5   35.9 / 31.2 ms   ← 两个方法都判异常
    32	cap3_hc   27.3 / 24.5 ms        bres   34.2 / 31.3 ms   ← 都判第二异常
    33	cap2      26.9 / 22.8 ms        hz1    28.5 / 24.6 ms
    34	foyer_hc  28.2 / 23.8 ms
    35	```
    36	
    37	**方法一** = busy 时间 × **busy 期间条件**在跑均值 ÷ 产出 token（从 `metrics.csv` + `steps.jsonl`）。⚠ 2026-09-23 修正：原来用的是全时段均值，等于把空转采样也算进去，τ 系统性偏低一个 busy 占比（cap1 从 28.8 变 18.5）
    38	**方法二** = 逐请求 `(total_duration_ms − first_token_ms) / (output_len_actual − 1)`
    39	
    40	**系统性差 8–16%，排序完全一致。**
    41	**反例检查**：这正是审计要查的——如果两者不相关，C2 就该删。它们相关。
    42	**⚠ 未解决**：`metrics.csv` 里 `itl_p50/p90`、`e2e_p50`、`ttft_p50` **全是空的**，
    43	引擎侧的延迟指标从未被采集。**方法二是替代品，不是引擎直采。**
    44	
    45	**状态：可用，但必须写成"两个导出量互相印证"，不能写成"引擎实测步时间"。**
    46	
    47	---
    48	
    49	## C3. 引擎空转是结构性的，占墙钟 7.4%–36.3%
    50	
    51	**数字**：`cap4 7.4% / cap3 9.9% / foyer 15.9% / bres 10.1% / cap2 18.4% / cap1 36.3%`
    52	段长：中位 5–10 s，p90 20–25 s，最长 366–386 s
    53	**成因**：会话在 `tool_wait` 期间不发请求 + 存活会话只有 ~2 个
    54	**对照**：并发越低空转越多（cap1 36.3% → cap4 7.4%），单调
    55	**反例检查**：`bres` 把空转压到 10.1% 却没变快 → **证明"填空转"本身不产生收益**，这条主张必须带上这个反例
    56	**路径**：`metrics.csv` 的 `num_running_reqs == 0` 连续段累加
    57	
    58	**状态：可用。必须同时报 `bres` 的反例，否则会被误读成"填上就能快"。**

    86	## C5. 四条机制上不同的政策路线，逐一否证且各有原因
    87	
    88	```
    89	改记账底座    charge=delta（§四十八，源码） + base=resident（§五十六，端到端）
    90	改增长预留    horizon 0/1/2/3 全扫（§五十七）
    91	改硬上限      target 各种组合
    92	改执行粒度    round-gate 限在跑数（§五十八，机制上不可实现）
    93	```
    94	
    95	**每条都成功地抬高了在跑数**（bres 引擎占用 41%→52%；hz1 并发 2.06→2.60），**但都不产生端到端收益。**
    96	**反例检查**：这正是"反例检查"本身——每条都是对前一条的否证尝试。
    97	**路径**：各 run 的 `controller.jsonl` + `metadata.json`
    98	
    99	**状态：可用。这是"边界"叙事的核心。**

   194	| **高** | 修复版 Foyer 与 cap3+HC 的配对复跑 | 3–9 小时 |
   195	| 中 | 驱逐政策对照（`slru` vs `lru`），C9 的边界 | 4 小时 |
   196	| 中 | 换配置复现（A100 或更大模型）——**分水岭，见下** | 1–2 天 |
   197	| 低 | 补采引擎侧 ITL（`metrics.csv` 那几列为什么是空的） | 待查 |
   198	
   199	**阻塞项**：卡被 `cjy` 占用，全部实验暂停。**上面两条"高"加起来只要 1 小时——卡一空就能补。**

     1	# 墙钟的两段分解 + 步时间 —— 由 scripts/lab/gen_decomp.py 生成，勿手改
     2	# busy/idle: 相邻采样按前一时刻 sglang:num_running_reqs 是否为 0 归类；busy+idle==span（有断言）
     3	# span_min: metrics.csv 的采样跨度；wall_min: run_summary 的墙钟。两者差 ~6 分，是采样边界

     1	#!/usr/bin/env python3
     2	"""前缀复用的天花板 vs 内存梯队 —— paper/data/reuse.csv。
     3	
     4	这条是把 §III 的 token 账从「驱逐→重算」推进到「梯队决定复用能不能兑现」。
     5	出发点是一个此前没被拆开的量：**总复用** = 设备侧命中 + host 侧接住。
     6	
     7	从 paper/data/eviction.csv 读（那三个量已在那里带断言算出），本脚本只做派生，
     8	不重新数日志 —— 避免同一个量在两处各算一遍然后对不上。
     9	
    10	```
    11	device-only（池子 = 101432 token，无 host 梯队）
    12	  cap1        R̄ 1.00   复用 19.9 M   重算  8.6 M
    13	  cap2        R̄ 1.27   复用 19.3 M   重算  9.2 M
    14	  cap3_fixed  R̄ 1.53   复用 17.0 M   重算 11.5 M
    15	  cap4        R̄ 1.65   复用 13.1 M   重算 15.4 M
    16	  cap5        R̄ 1.84   复用  7.8 M   重算 20.7 M   <- 天花板兑现不了
    17	
    18	HiCache（设备池同样 101432，另有 host 梯队）
    19	  pairB_cap2hc R̄ 1.25  复用 19.9 M   重算  8.6 M
    20	  cap3_hc      R̄ 1.51  复用 19.6 M   重算  8.9 M
    21	  cap4_hc      R̄ 1.73  复用 19.4 M   重算  9.1 M   <- 天花板守住了
    22	```
    23	
    24	**天花板本身是 trace 的性质**（该 trace 前缀结构允许的复用上限 ≈ 19.9 M，对应计划
    25	命中率 ~70%），**由谁兑现是配置的性质**。device-only 在 R̄ 过了 1.3 就兑现不了，
    26	HiCache 在控制器整个运行区间内都兑现。
    27	
    28	注意 HiCache 的**设备侧命中反而更少**（cap4_hc 设备未命中 22.4 M，比 device-only 的
    29	cap4 的 15.4 M 还多）—— 设备 miss 多而复用不少，差额正是 host 接住的 13.3 M。
    30	所以「看设备命中率」会得出与「看复用」相反的方向，这也是为什么这一列必须单列。
    31	
    32	用法: python3 gen_reuse.py paper/data/eviction.csv paper/data/arm_running.csv \
    33	                        paper/data/decomp.csv paper/data/reuse.csv
    34	"""
    35	import csv, os, sys
    36	
    37	
    38	def load(p):
    39	    return {r["run"]: r for r in csv.DictReader(l for l in open(p) if not l.startswith("#"))}
    40	
    41	
    42	def main():
    43	    ev_p, ar_p, dc_p, out_p = sys.argv[1:5]
    44	    ev, ar, dc = load(ev_p), load(ar_p), load(dc_p)
    45	    # 争用标记：被主机争用抬高了 tau 的 run 不能拿 tau 列当证据。
    46	    # 复用列（reuse_*/recompute_M）是【体积比】，与时间无关，不受争用影响 ——
    47	    # 实测这四条臂的 reuse 与同族干净 run 一致（0.691~0.694），只有 tau 被抬高。
    48	    conc = {}
    49	    if len(sys.argv) > 5 and os.path.exists(sys.argv[5]):
    50	        # 注意去掉 pois200_ 前缀再配对：eviction.csv 用的是短名（cap1 / foyer_hz2），
    51	        # concurrency.csv 用的是 run 目录名（pois200_cap1 / pois200_foyer_hz2）。
    52	        # 不归一化会静默全部落空，contested 列全变成 "?"（2026-09-24 犯过一次）。
    53	        conc = {r["run"].replace("pois200_", ""): r["verdict"] for r in
    54	                csv.DictReader(l for l in open(sys.argv[5]) if not l.startswith("#"))}
    55	        assert len(conc) > 50, "concurrency.csv 只读到 %d 行，路径或格式不对" % len(conc)
    56	
    57	    rows, bad = [], []
    58	    for run, e in ev.items():
    59	        a, d = ar.get(run), dc.get(run)
    60	        if not a or not d:
    61	            bad.append((run, "arm_running 或 decomp 里没有这条"))
    62	            continue
    63	        prompt = float(e["prompt_M"])
    64	        dev_miss = float(e["dev_miss_M"])
    65	        host_hit = float(e["host_hit_M"])
    66	        unc = float(e["uncached_M"])
    67	        reuse_dev = prompt - dev_miss
    68	        reuse_tot = reuse_dev + host_hit
    69	
    70	        # 断言 1：设备未命中 = host 接住 + 真正重算。这是这套记账的定义式，
    71	        # 挡住的是从 eviction.csv 抄错列（抄错不会有这个性质）。
    72	        assert abs(dev_miss - (host_hit + unc)) < 0.15, \
    73	            "%s: dev_miss %.1f != host_hit %.1f + uncached %.1f" % (run, dev_miss, host_hit, unc)
    74	        # 断言 2：复用 + 重算 = 总 prompt
    75	        assert abs(reuse_tot + unc - prompt) < 0.15, \
    76	            "%s: reuse %.1f + uncached %.1f != prompt %.1f" % (run, reuse_tot, unc, prompt)
    77	
    78	        rows.append({
    79	            "run": run,
    80	            "family": "hicache" if e["hicache"] == "1" else "device_only",
    81	            "Rbar_busy": a["running_busy"],
    82	            "prompt_M": "%.1f" % prompt,
    83	            "reuse_dev_M": "%.1f" % reuse_dev,
    84	            "reuse_host_M": "%.1f" % host_hit,
    85	            "reuse_total_M": "%.1f" % reuse_tot,
    86	            "reuse_frac": "%.3f" % (reuse_tot / prompt),
    87	            "recompute_M": "%.1f" % unc,
    88	            "tau1_ms": d["step_back_ms"],
    89	            "contested": conc.get(run, "?"),
    90	        })
    91	
    92	    rows.sort(key=lambda r: (r["family"], float(r["Rbar_busy"])))
    93	    header = "\n".join([
    94	        "# 前缀复用的天花板 vs 内存梯队 —— 由 scripts/lab/gen_reuse.py 生成，勿手改",
    95	        "# reuse_dev_M   = prompt - dev_miss       设备侧 radix cache 兑现的复用",
    96	        "# reuse_host_M  = host 梯队接住的（device-only 恒为 0）",
    97	        "# reuse_total_M = 两者之和 = 真正没有重算的那部分 prompt",
    98	        "# reuse_frac    = reuse_total / prompt —— device-only 随 R̄ 塌，hicache 守住",
    99	        "# 天花板（trace 允许的最大复用）≈ 19.9 M；兑现它与否是【配置】的属性",
   100	        "# contested: 该 run 是否落在主机争用窗口里（见 concurrency.csv）。CONTESTED 的",
   101	        "#            tau1_ms 列不可用；reuse_* 是体积比，与时间无关，不受争用影响。",
   102	        "run,family,Rbar_busy,prompt_M,reuse_dev_M,reuse_host_M,reuse_total_M,reuse_frac,recompute_M,tau1_ms,contested"])
   103	    fields = ["run", "family", "Rbar_busy", "prompt_M", "reuse_dev_M", "reuse_host_M",
   104	              "reuse_total_M", "reuse_frac", "recompute_M", "tau1_ms", "contested"]
   105	    body = header + "\n" + "\n".join(",".join(str(r[k]) for k in fields) for r in rows) + "\n"
   106	    assert isinstance(body, str)
   107	    with open(out_p, "w") as fh:
   108	        fh.write(body)
   109	
   110	    print("写入 %s：%d 行" % (out_p, len(rows)))
   111	    for r in rows:
   112	        print("  %-16s %-11s R̄ %-6s 复用 %5s M (%.3f)  重算 %5s M  τ₁ %-6s %s" % (
   113	            r["run"], r["family"], r["Rbar_busy"], r["reuse_total_M"],
   114	            float(r["reuse_frac"]), r["recompute_M"], r["tau1_ms"],
   115	            "<<CONTESTED" if r["contested"] == "CONTESTED" else ""))
   116	    if bad:
   117	        print("!! 未写入：")
   118	        for n, why in bad:
   119	            print("   %-16s %s" % (n, why))
   120	
   121	
   122	if __name__ == "__main__":
   123	    main()
     1	#!/usr/bin/env python3
     2	"""并发争用审计 —— 每条 run 在生命周期内与几条别的 run 真正重叠，以及它的 τ₂。
     3	
     4	为什么必须做这个：2026-09-24 发现，同机同时跑 >=3 台 SGLang server 会把【逐请求
     5	decode 间隔中位 τ₂】抬高 25~40%。τ₂ 是客户端实测的、不经采样器的量，所以这不是
     6	采样伪影，是真实的步成本上升。受影响 run 的墙钟与 SLO 与其余各行不可比。
     7	
     8	证据链（本脚本输出 + probe_concurrency.py 的时间分箱）：
     9	  - cap1 在 1~2 条车道时 τ₂ 恒为 20.3~20.7；02:25 rl90/rl99 起来（3 条）后跳到 22.4~27.5
    10	  - t85 与 rl99 两条独立 run 在 07:09~07:23（rl90 结束前后）同时从 ~30 回落到 ~22~25
    11	  - bres/hz0/hz2/breshz1 四条彼此重叠约 300 分钟（4 条车道），τ₂ 30.9~33.3，
    12	    而同族干净 run 是 23.8~25.7
    13	
    14	判据（看数据前定死）：**同时活着的 run 数 >=4 且持续 >=60 分钟** 记 CONTESTED。
    15	两个限定都是必须的：
    16	  - 用「同时数」而不是「重叠对象数+1」——本项目是链式车道，上一条结束下一条就起，
    17	    按对象数会把顺序相接的 2 条误报成 3 条（2026-09-24 犯过，脚本第一版就错在这）。
    18	  - 用「持续时长」而不是峰值——foyer_hz1 峰值到过 4 条但只有 14 分钟，τ₂=24.6 正常。
    19	
    20	实测 2~3 条不抬 τ₂（pairB_foyerhc 3 条 277 分钟 τ₂=23.8；cap3_fixed 3 条 163 分钟
    21	τ₂=24.9；sf0_r2/hz1_r2/cap3_hc_r2 都在 3 条下跑，τ₂ 24.3~25.0），4 条才抬。
    22	注意判定是"该 run 的测量窗口里有持续争用"，是保守触发条件；τ₂ 一列是实际是否被抬高的
    23	读数，两者一起看——t85/t90/var_foyer 触发了 CONTESTED 但 τ₂ 正常（只有前半段被抬）。
    24	
    25	用法: python3 gen_concurrency.py <results_dir> <out.csv>
    26	"""
    27	import csv, glob, json, os, statistics, sys
    28	
    29	OVERLAP_MIN_S = 600.0
    30	EXPECT_TAU2 = True
    31	
    32	
    33	def tau2(root, run):
    34	    """逐请求 decode 间隔中位（与 gen_decomp.py 的口径一致）。"""
    35	    p = os.path.join(root, run, "steps.jsonl")
    36	    if not os.path.exists(p):
    37	        return None
    38	    try:
    39	        steps = [json.loads(l) for l in open(p) if l.strip()]
    40	    except Exception:
    41	        return None
    42	    ok = [s for s in steps
    43	          if (s.get("output_len_actual") or 0) >= 32 and s.get("status") == "SUCCESS"]
    44	    if not ok:
    45	        return None
    46	    return statistics.median(
    47	        (s["total_duration_ms"] - s["first_token_ms"]) / max(1, (s["output_len_actual"] - 1))
    48	        for s in ok)
    49	
    50	
    51	def main():
    52	    root, out_path = sys.argv[1], sys.argv[2]
    53	    rows = []
    54	    for d in glob.glob(os.path.join(root, "*", "metadata.json")):
    55	        try:
    56	            m = json.load(open(d))
    57	        except Exception:
    58	            continue
    59	        s, f = m.get("started"), m.get("finished")
    60	        if not s or not f:
    61	            continue
    62	        rows.append({"run": os.path.basename(os.path.dirname(d)),
    63	                     "started": s, "finished": f, "gpu": m.get("gpu")})
    64	    rows.sort(key=lambda r: r["started"])
    65	
    66	    # 关键：并发数必须是【同时活着的条数】，不是"重叠对象数+1"。
    67	    # 后者会把顺序相接的链式车道当成同时（本项目是链式：上一条结束下一条就开始），
    68	    # 于是把 2 条误报成 3 条。这里用事件扫描算真实同时数，再统计【持续多久】。
    69	    def lanes_over_time(lo, hi):
    70	        """返回 (峰值同时数, 同时数>=4 的总秒数, 同时数>=3 的总秒数)。"""
    71	        ev = []
    72	        for o in rows:
    73	            a, b = max(lo, o["started"]), min(hi, o["finished"])
    74	            if b > a:
    75	                ev.append((a, 1))
    76	                ev.append((b, -1))
    77	        ev.sort()
    78	        cur = peak = 0
    79	        ge4 = ge3 = 0.0
    80	        prev = lo
    81	        for t, d in ev:
    82	            if cur >= 4:
    83	                ge4 += t - prev
    84	            if cur >= 3:
    85	                ge3 += t - prev
    86	            cur += d
    87	            peak = max(peak, cur)
    88	            prev = t
    89	        return peak, ge4, ge3
    90	
    91	    for r in rows:
    92	        peak, ge4, ge3 = lanes_over_time(r["started"], r["finished"])
    93	        r["peak_lanes"] = peak
    94	        r["min_at_ge4"] = round(ge4 / 60.0, 1)
    95	        r["min_at_ge3"] = round(ge3 / 60.0, 1)
    96	        # 判据定在 >=4 条【持续】超过 60 分钟：实测 2~3 条的 run 的 τ₂ 都在同族正常范围
    97	        # （pairB_foyerhc 3 条 277 分钟 τ₂=23.8；cap3_fixed 3 条 163 分钟 τ₂=24.9），
    98	        # 而 4 条的（bres/hz0/hz2/breshz1，各约 300 分钟）τ₂ 30.9~33.3。
    99	        # 只看峰值不看时长会把 hz1（峰值 4 但只有 14 分钟）误判，故按时长判。
   100	        r["verdict"] = "CONTESTED" if r["min_at_ge4"] >= 60.0 else (
   101	            "watch" if r["min_at_ge3"] >= 60.0 else "clean")
   102	        r["tau2_ms"] = tau2(root, r["run"])
   103	
   104	    header = "\n".join([
   105	        "# 并发争用审计 —— 由 scripts/lab/gen_concurrency.py 生成，勿手改",
   106	        "# peak_lanes: 生命周期内同时活着的 run 数峰值（事件扫描，不是重叠对象数+1）",
   107	        "# min_at_ge4: 同时 >=4 条持续的分钟数 —— 判据用它，因为只有持续争用才抬 τ₂",
   108	        "# verdict: min_at_ge4>=60 记 CONTESTED（墙钟/SLO 与其余各行不可比，须重测）；",
   109	        "#          min_at_ge3>=60 记 watch（实测 3 条无一致抬升，但记录在案）",
   110	        "# tau2_ms: 逐请求 decode 间隔中位（客户端实测，不经采样器）；争用会把它抬高 ~25~40%",
   111	        "run,peak_lanes,min_at_ge4,min_at_ge3,tau2_ms,verdict"])
   112	    fields = ["run", "peak_lanes", "min_at_ge4", "min_at_ge3", "tau2_ms", "verdict"]
   113	    # 一次写成一个 str（本项目教训：open(p,"w") 先截断后报错，内容非 str 会把文件清空）
   114	    lines = [header]
   115	    for r in rows:
   116	        lines.append(",".join(str(r[k]) for k in fields))
   117	    body = "\n".join(lines) + "\n"
   118	    assert isinstance(body, str)
   119	    with open(out_path, "w") as fh:
   120	        fh.write(body)
   121	
   122	    nbad = sum(1 for r in rows if r["verdict"] == "CONTESTED")
   123	    nw = sum(1 for r in rows if r["verdict"] == "watch")
   124	    print("写入 %s：%d 行，CONTESTED %d 行，watch %d 行" % (out_path, len(rows), nbad, nw))
   125	    for r in rows:
   126	        if r["verdict"] == "CONTESTED":
   127	            print("  CONTESTED %-30s 峰值%d条  >=4条共%.0f分钟  τ₂=%.1f" % (
   128	                r["run"], r["peak_lanes"], r["min_at_ge4"], r["tau2_ms"] or float("nan")))
   129	
   130	
   131	if __name__ == "__main__":
   132	    main()
     1	#!/usr/bin/env python3
     2	"""把 run 放到静态前沿上判「赢了没有」。
     3	
     4	用法:  python3 frontier.py <run> [<run> ...]
     5	       python3 frontier.py --all-foyer
     6	
     7	为什么需要它：我们反复在"这个数比那个数好"上打转，而正确的判据只有一个——
     8	**这个点在不在这条 (墙钟, SLO) 静态前沿的上方**。落在两个静态 cap 中间的连线上
     9	不算赢，因为静态方法按时间片混合就能逼近线上任一点（§二十七、§五十二）。
    10	
    11	前沿的三个锚点（全部 999 步、HiCache 开、同一代码代）：
    12	    cap4_hc_r2 (209.1, 68.5)   # 重复 run 支配了 cap4_hc，见 paper/data/runs.csv
    13	    cap3_hc_r2 (230.3, 81.9)   # 2026-09-24 更正：cap3_hc 的重复同样在两个轴上都支配它，
    14	                               # 按同一条规矩必须顶掉。原稿以"它超了自己的 cap"为由排除，
    15	                               # 但那是【另一条非 HiCache 的 pois200_cap3_r2】（峰值在飞 5、
    16	                               # 超帽 126 个样本）；cap3_hc_r2 峰值在飞 3、超帽 0，是干净的。
    17	    cap2_hc  (295.0, 91.6)   <- pairB；pairA (304.5, 90.6) 被它支配，不入前沿
    18	低于 213 分钟没有任何静态点，落在那里是平凡获胜。
    19	"""
    20	import json, os, statistics as st, sys
    21	
    22	RESULTS = "/data/xbw/turnstile/results/night"
    23	# 上左包络的锚点，按墙钟升序。cap1 没有 HiCache 版本，但它仍然是"静态 cap 能达到的点"，
    24	# 必须放进前沿——否则墙钟超过最右锚点的 run 会被误判成"越过前沿"（cap1 自己就撞过这个）。
    25	FRONT = [(209.1, 68.5, "cap4_hc_r2"), (230.3, 81.9, "cap3_hc_r2"),
    26	         (295.0, 91.6, "cap2_hc"), (527.4, 94.8, "cap1")]
    27	
    28	
    29	def front_slo(wall):
    30	    """前沿在给定墙钟处的 SLO 上界（分段线性插值）。超出锚点范围返回 None——
    31	    范围外没有可比的前沿，不能给判决，否则端点外推会凭空造出"越过"。"""
    32	    if wall < FRONT[0][0] or wall > FRONT[-1][0]:
    33	        return None
    34	    for (w0, s0, _), (w1, s1, _) in zip(FRONT, FRONT[1:]):
    35	        if w0 <= wall <= w1:
    36	            return s0 + (s1 - s0) * (wall - w0) / (w1 - w0)
    37	    return None
    38	
    39	
    40	def summarise(run):
    41	    p = os.path.join(RESULTS, run, "steps.jsonl")
    42	    if not os.path.exists(p):
    43	        return None
    44	    rows = [json.loads(l) for l in open(p)]
    45	    ts = [r["submit_timestamp"] for r in rows if r.get("submit_timestamp")]
    46	    te = [r["complete_timestamp"] for r in rows if r.get("complete_timestamp")]
    47	    tt = [r["first_token_ms"] for r in rows if r.get("first_token_ms") is not None]
    48	    if not ts or not tt:
    49	        return None
    50	    wall = (max(te) - min(ts)) / 60.0
    51	    slo = 100.0 * sum(1 for x in tt if x <= 10000) / len(tt)
    52	    # 实测并发：按 30 秒采样活跃请求数
    53	    N = 300
    54	    act = []
    55	    for i in range(N):
    56	        t = min(ts) + (max(te) - min(ts)) * i / (N - 1)
    57	        act.append(sum(1 for r in rows if r.get("submit_timestamp") and r.get("complete_timestamp")
    58	                       and r["submit_timestamp"] <= t < r["complete_timestamp"]))
    59	    # 分段 SLO 标准差（十等分）
    60	    seg = []
    61	    for i in range(10):
    62	        s = tt[i * len(tt) // 10:(i + 1) * len(tt) // 10]
    63	        if s:
    64	            seg.append(100.0 * sum(1 for x in s if x <= 10000) / len(s))
    65	    sd = st.pstdev(seg) if len(seg) > 1 else 0.0
    66	    return dict(run=run, steps=len(rows), wall=wall, conc=sum(act) / N, slo=slo, sd=sd)
    67	
    68	
    69	def verdict(d):
    70	    f = front_slo(d["wall"])
    71	    d["front"] = float("nan") if f is None else f
    72	    if f is None:
    73	        d["delta"] = float("nan")
    74	        d["verdict"] = ("LEFT-OF-FRONT (无静态点在更左, 平凡获胜)"
    75	                        if d["wall"] < FRONT[0][0] else "OUT-OF-RANGE (超出现有静态点范围)")
    76	        return d
    77	    d["delta"] = d["slo"] - f
    78	    if d["delta"] > 1.0:
    79	        d["verdict"] = "ABOVE  (越过前沿)"
    80	    elif d["delta"] > -1.0:
    81	        d["verdict"] = "ON     (在线上, 不算赢)"
    82	    else:
    83	        d["verdict"] = "BELOW  (在线下)"
    84	    return d
    85	
    86	
    87	def main():
    88	    runs = sys.argv[1:]
    89	    if runs == ["--all-foyer"]:
    90	        runs = sorted(r for r in os.listdir(RESULTS)
    91	                      if r.startswith("pois200_foyer") or r.startswith("pois200_cap"))
    92	    out = []
    93	    for r in runs:
    94	        d = summarise(r)
    95	        if d and d["steps"] >= 999:
    96	            out.append(verdict(d))
    97	    print("%-30s %5s %8s %7s %7s %8s %8s  %s" %
    98	          ("run", "步数", "墙钟min", "并发", "SLO%", "前沿SLO", "差(pp)", "判决"))
    99	    for d in sorted(out, key=lambda x: x["wall"]):
   100	        print("%-30s %5d %8.1f %7.2f %7.1f %8.1f %+8.2f  %s" %
   101	              (d["run"], d["steps"], d["wall"], d["conc"], d["slo"], d["front"], d["delta"], d["verdict"]))
   102	    print("\n注意：步数 < 999 的 run 一律不列（未跑完的不能与跑完的比，§多处的教训）。")
   103	
   104	
   105	if __name__ == "__main__":
   106	    main()
     1	#!/usr/bin/env python3
     2	"""从 runs.csv 生成论文表 I 的 LaTeX 表体 —— 让「每个判决都由一个脚本产出」成为真的。
     3	
     4	为什么必须有：表 I 的 Δ 列原来是手抄的，其中两行抄错了（rg4 写成 −24.0 实际
     5	−25.02；foyfix 写成 −48.7 实际 −47.15）。而正文第 §7 声称每个判决都由单一脚本
     6	从原始日志产出 —— 手抄的表让这句话不成立。这个脚本把表变成可复现的。
     7	
     8	判决口径与 scripts/lab/frontier.py 一致：
     9	  包络 = 静态点（含 +HC）的上左包络
    10	  Δ = 该点的 SLO − 包络在同墙钟处的插值 SLO
    11	  判读：Δ > +1.0 越过 / −1.0 ≤ Δ ≤ +1.0 平局 / Δ < −1.0 在线下
    12	
    13	用法: python3 gen_table.py ../paper/data/runs.csv
    14	"""
    15	import csv, sys
    16	
    17	# 表里的行标签（与论文一致），以及它们的排序位置由 Δ 决定
    18	LABEL = {
    19	    "default": "no control",
    20	    "cap1": "cap1", "cap2": "cap2", "cap3_fixed": "cap3 (fixed)",
    21	    "cap4": "cap4", "cap5": "cap5",
    22	    "pairB_cap2hc": "cap2+HC", "cap3_hc": "cap3+HC", "cap4_hc": "cap4+HC",
    23	    "cap4_hc_r2": "cap4+HC (repeat)", "cap3_hc_r2": "cap3+HC (repeat)",
    24	    "foyer_hc_r2": "horizon$=$3 (2nd run)", "foyer_sf0_r2": "single-flight off",
    25	    "foyer_hz1": "horizon$=$1",
    26	    "foyer_hz1_r2": "horizon$=$1 (repeat)", "foyer_hc": "horizon$=$3 (base)",
    27	    "foyer_hz2": "horizon$=$2", "foyer_bres": "resident base",
    28	    "foyer_hz0": "horizon$=$0", "foyer_breshz1": "horizon$=$1, no hw",
    29	    "foyer_nogate": "no gate, no resv.", "foyer_rg4": "running gate$=$4",
    30	    "foyfix_hc": "all throttles off",
    31	    "aimd2": "AIMD, retuned", "aimd2_paper": "AIMD, paper params",
    32	}
    33	ENV_KEYS = ["cap4_hc", "cap4_hc_r2", "cap3_hc", "cap3_hc_r2", "pairB_cap2hc", "cap1"]  # 候选锚点（由上左包络自动筛）
    34	
    35	
    36	def load(p):
    37	    return list(csv.DictReader(l for l in open(p) if not l.startswith("#")))
    38	
    39	
    40	def _envelope(keys, runs):
    41	    """候选锚点的上左包络：按墙钟升序扫，SLO 破纪录的才留在前沿上。"""
    42	    out, best = [], -1
    43	    for k in sorted(keys, key=lambda k: float(runs[k]["wall_min"])):
    44	        s = float(runs[k]["slo_pct"])
    45	        if s > best:
    46	            out.append((float(runs[k]["wall_min"]), s, k)); best = s
    47	    return out
    48	
    49	
    50	def main():
    51	    runs = {r["run"]: r for r in load(sys.argv[1]) if r["tier"] == "200"}
    52	    env = [(w, s) for w, s, _ in _envelope(ENV_KEYS, runs)]
    53	
    54	    def front_slo(w):
    55	        for (w0, s0), (w1, s1) in zip(env, env[1:]):
    56	            if w0 <= w <= w1:
    57	                return s0 + (w - w0) / (w1 - w0) * (s1 - s0)
    58	        return None
    59	
    60	    def delta(k):
    61	        r = runs[k]
    62	        f = front_slo(float(r["wall_min"]))
    63	        return None if f is None else float(r["slo_pct"]) - f
    64	
    65	    def row(k, anchor=False):
    66	        r = runs[k]
    67	        d = delta(k)
    68	        ds = "\\emph{anchor}" if anchor else ("$+$%.2f" % d if d > 0 else "$-$%.2f" % -d)
    69	        if anchor:
    70	            ds = "\\emph{anchor}"
    71	        return "    %-17s & %s & %s & %s & %s \\\\" % (
    72	            LABEL[k], r["wall_min"], r["conc"], r["slo_pct"], ds)
    73	
    74	    print("    \\multicolumn{5}{l}{\\emph{Static caps}}\\\\")
    75	    for k in ["default", "cap1", "cap2", "cap3_fixed", "cap4", "cap5"]:
    76	        print(row(k, anchor=(k == "cap1")))
    77	    print("    \\midrule")
    78	    print("    \\multicolumn{5}{l}{\\emph{Static caps + HiCache}}\\\\")
    79	    # 只把【真正落在上左包络上】的点标成 anchor —— 不能按 ENV_KEYS 一律标，
    80	    # 因为 cap4_hc 已经被它自己的重复支配了，它不在前沿上。
    81	    on_front = {n for _, _, n in _envelope(ENV_KEYS, runs)}
    82	    for k in ["pairB_cap2hc", "cap3_hc", "cap3_hc_r2", "cap4_hc", "cap4_hc_r2"]:
    83	        print(row(k, anchor=(k in on_front)))
    84	    print("    \\midrule")
    85	    print("    \\multicolumn{5}{l}{\\emph{\\sys{} controller variants}}\\\\")
    86	    foyer = sorted([k for k in LABEL if k.startswith("foyer") or k == "foyfix_hc"],
    87	                   key=lambda k: -delta(k))
    88	    for k in foyer:
    89	        print(row(k))
    90	    print("    \\midrule")
    91	    print("    \\multicolumn{5}{l}{\\emph{Independent adaptive controller (\\S\\ref{sec:results:aimd})}}\\\\")
    92	    for k in sorted(["aimd2", "aimd2_paper"], key=lambda k: -delta(k)):
    93	        print(row(k))
    94	
    95	
    96	if __name__ == "__main__":
    97	    main()
