 succeeded in 0ms:
     1	# 发现：同机 ≥4 条车道会把步成本抬高 25~40%，四条控制器臂的数据作废
     2	
     3	**日期** 2026-09-24 · **脚本** `scripts/lab/gen_concurrency.py`（审计）、
     4	`scripts/lab/probe_concurrency.py`（时间分箱） · **数据** `paper/data/concurrency.csv`
     5	
     6	## 怎么发现的
     7	
     8	在做方向 B（把「host tier 决定准入控制有没有空间」做成主线）时，去查论文
     9	§III 自己标为「the largest open question」的那件事：HiCache 族的九条臂
    10	**重算量几乎恒定（8.6~9.1 M），但 τ₁ 却在 27.0~35.7 ms 之间散开**，论文原话是
    11	"Whatever moves τ there is not recomputation, and we do not know what it is."
    12	
    13	把八条 HiCache 臂的 τ₂（**逐请求 decode 间隔中位**，由 per-request 的
    14	`(total_duration − first_token)/(output_len − 1)` 算出，客户端实测、**不经过采样器**）
    15	按 R̄ 排开之后，出现了一个论文没注意到的分裂：
    16	
    17	| 组 | 臂 | τ₂ |
    18	|---|---|---|
    19	| 正常 | cap4_hc / cap3_hc / foyer_hc / foyer_hz1 / foyer_sf0_r2 | 23.8 ~ 25.7 |
    20	| **抬高** | **foyer_bres / foyer_hz0 / foyer_hz2 / foyer_breshz1** | **30.9 ~ 33.3** |
    21	
    22	bres（R̄ 1.481）与 cap3_hc（R̄ 1.509）、foyer_hz1（R̄ 1.524）**连占用率都匹配**
    23	（54.3% vs 55.5% / 54.1%），却慢 6.7 ms。四条高 τ₂ 的臂**恰好就是** 09-22 23:17–23:33
    24	同一分钟启动、彼此重叠约 300 分钟的那一批（详见下表），当时四台 SGLang server
    25	同机运行。
    26	
    27	## 判据与验证
    28	
    29	### 1. 不是时间局部的（排除"跑到一半有人挤进来"）
    30	
    31	把每条 run 的 τ₂ 沿时间分 6~16 箱取中位（`probe_concurrency.py`）：
    32	
    33	```
    34	foyer_hz2     33.0 33.1 30.5 31.1 27.9 24.6    全程高
    35	foyer_hz0     34.0 33.8 34.5 30.4 32.5 33.7    全程高
    36	foyer_breshz1 33.5 33.9 34.1 33.9 33.3 28.8    全程高
    37	foyer_hc      24.1 24.4 23.8 22.8 23.6 24.1    全程低
    38	cap4_hc       25.5 27.2 25.4 25.6 26.1 25.5    全程低
    39	```
    40	
    41	外部进程**中途挤进来**会表现为某几箱出现台阶。这里是平的，即**整个测量窗口都被
    42	同一个持续条件覆盖**——与"四条车道自始至终互相争用"一致。
    43	
    44	### 2. 对照实验 1：cap1 在车道数变化处的跳变
    45	
    46	`pois200_cap1`（20:13 起跑）独自跑了 5.5 小时，02:25 rl90/rl99 同时启动：
    47	
    48	```
    49	20:37 20:10 21:43 22:16 22:49 23:22 23:55 00:28 01:01 01:34 02:07 | 02:40 03:13 03:46 04:18 04:51
    50	20.5  20.3  21.2  20.6  20.4  20.6  20.4  20.4  20.6  20.5  20.7  | 22.4  27.5  20.9  25.4  24.0
    51	        └──────────── 1~2 条车道，恒在 20.3~20.7 ────────────┘        └── 3~4 条车道 ──┘
    52	```
    53	
    54	### 3. 对照实验 2：t85 与 rl99 在同一分钟同时回落
    55	
    56	两条**独立**的 run，τ₂ 在 07:09~07:23（rl90 结束前后）**同一时刻**从 ~30 掉到 ~22~25：
    57	
    58	```
    59	t85   30.0 25.8 30.9 25.1 31.0 29.5 30.3 | 22.3 23.7 22.6 22.0 22.4 23.4 23.5 24.3 23.4
    60	t90   29.6 25.1 30.2 27.1 30.5 27.3      | 21.4 23.6 21.9 22.2 23.4 23.1 23.5 23.4
    61	rl99  33.0 37.5 32.9 31.4 28.9 30.2 35.0 30.8 35.3 31.0 39.7 31.6 30.4 | 25.3 24.6
    62	                                        └── 07:09~07:23 同时回落 ──┘
    63	```
    64	
    65	两个独立的测量在同一个绝对时刻改变水平，只能是**共同的外部条件**，不可能是各自的
    66	工作负载或策略变化。
    67	
    68	### 4. 剂量：2~3 条不抬，4 条才抬
    69	
    70	| 并发 | 实例 | τ₂ |
    71	|---|---|---|
    72	| 2 条 | 绝大多数 run | 同族正常 |
    73	| 3 条 | `pairB_foyerhc`（277 min）、`cap3_fixed`（163 min）、`foyer_sf0_r2`、`foyer_hz1_r2`、`cap3_hc_r2` | 23.8 ~ 25.0，**均正常** |
    74	| **4 条** | `bres`/`hz0`/`hz2`/`breshz1`（各约 296 min） | **30.9 ~ 33.3** |
    75	| 4 条 | `_failed_4lane_*`（09-24 被 SIGTERM 的两条） | 31.4 / 32.1 |
    76	
    77	这一条正是 `CLAUDE.md` 里 2026-09-21 和 2026-09-24 两次踩到的同一个坑
    78	（"不要同时占满四张卡"，四车道会被别的租户的 reaper 杀掉）。当时的结论是
    79	"会被杀"，**这次发现的是更早的代价：即使没被杀，数据也已经不可比了**。
    80	
    81	## 审计结果
    82	
    83	`paper/data/concurrency.csv`（123 条 run）：
    84	
    85	- **CONTESTED（同时 ≥4 条持续 ≥60 分钟）12 条**
    86	- watch（≥3 条持续 ≥60 分钟）4 条
    87	- clean 107 条
    88	
    89	表 I 里受影响的臂正好 4 条，全部是控制器变体：
    90	
    91	| run | 峰值 | ≥4 条持续 | τ₂ |
    92	|---|---|---|---|
    93	| `foyer_breshz1` | 4 | 296 分钟 | 33.3 |
    94	| `foyer_hz0` | 4 | 296 分钟 | 33.1 |
    95	| `foyer_bres` | 4 | 296 分钟 | 31.3 |
    96	| `foyer_hz2` | 4 | 282 分钟 | 30.9 |
    97	
    98	其余 CONTESTED 的 8 条都不在表 I：rl90 / rl99 / t85 / t90（rl 与 t 的扫描）、
    99	var_foyer / var_cap3（变负载族）、以及 09-24 被 SIGTERM 的两条 `_failed_4lane_*`。
   100	
   101	`pois200_cap1` 只有 28.8 分钟处于 ≥4 条，记 watch；它的 τ₂ 20.6 是全场最低，
   102	未受影响。
   103	
   104	## 影响估计（重测之前，只作量级参考，不进正文）
   105	
   106	τ 若按同族干净水平折算，这四条的忙时会缩短约 22%，墙钟大致：
   107	
   108	```
   109	foyer_bres    295.5 → ~247 分    对前沿在该点的插值 91.6  →  Δ 由 −7.8 变到约 −0.6
   110	```
   111	
   112	**如果这个量级成立，表 I 的判决会变**——这四条的 Δ 可能从"明显在线下"变成
   113	"贴着线"。所以必须重测，不能靠折算。
   114	
   115	## 已采取的动作
   116	
   117	1. **重跑这四条**（`lane_foyer_r2_batch1.sh` / `lane_foyer_r2_batch2.sh`），
   118	   新 run 名加 `_r2` 后缀，严格 2 条车道。旧目录原样保留，不覆盖不删除。
   119	2. 重跑用 `wait_and_launch.sh` 排队：等前一条车道的**最后一个 run 写出 summary**
   120	   （不是"显存空"——run_matrix 在一条车道的两条 run 之间会卸载 server，
   121	   那一刻显存是空的但车道没结束），启动前再数一次自己的 server 数。
   122	3. 审计脚本进仓，`paper/data/concurrency.csv` 由它生成。
   123	
   124	## 对论文的意义（要如实写）
   125	
   126	- **表 I 的这四行在重测前不可信**。重测完用新 run 替换，并在可复现性一节
   127	  说明为什么换、怎么发现的。
   128	- 这是一条**方法论发现**，本身值得写：内存受限 serving 的测量对同机并发
   129	  **高度敏感**，而敏感性只体现在 `τ₂` 这种**逐请求的客户端量**上——引擎侧
   130	  `num_running_reqs`、占用率、驱逐量、重算量**全都看不出异常**（这四条臂在这四列上
   131	  与干净 run 不可区分）。如果只盯着引擎指标，这批数据会被当成好的。
   132	- 与 §III 那条"最大未解问题"的关系：论文原来写"HiCache 族重算恒定而 τ 散开，
   133	  我们不知道为什么"。**其中一部分现在有答案了——那四条臂的 τ 不是被什么都西推动的，
   134	  是被同机争用推动的**。剩下五条（hc/hz1/hz2 之外的）重算恒定而 τ 在 27.0~29.2 之间
   135	  小范围移动，这是重测之后要重新面对的问题，范围比原来小得多。

     1	# 发现：判据曲线在控制器臂的实际配置下没有悬崖
     2	
     3	日期：2026-09-23
     4	脚本：`scripts/lab/gen_elasticity_hc.py` → `paper/data/elasticity_hc.csv`
     5	起因：codex 审稿意见 #1 —— 「分段弹性来自**无 HiCache 的五个静态 cap**，
     6	而九条控制器臂**全部开着 HiCache**；论文自己承认 HiCache 下相近运行数可对应
     7	明显不同的步成本。」
     8	
     9	## 两条曲线（同一个 tau 口径：busy 条件均值，见 gen_arm_running.py）
    10	
    11	```
    12	device-only（论文 §IV 现在唯一用的那条）
    13	  cap1         Rbar 1.00  tau 28.8
    14	  cap2         Rbar 1.27  tau 27.4     eps = -0.208
    15	  cap3_fixed   Rbar 1.53  tau 29.7     eps = +0.433
    16	  cap4         Rbar 1.65  tau 32.4     eps = +1.152   <-- 悬崖
    17	  cap5         Rbar 1.84  tau 36.7     eps = +1.143   <-- 悬崖
    18	
    19	HiCache（九条控制器臂实际运行的配置）
    20	  pairB_cap2hc Rbar 1.25  tau 27.0
    21	  cap3_hc      Rbar 1.51  tau 28.0     eps = +0.192
    22	  cap4_hc      Rbar 1.73  tau 29.8     eps = +0.458
    23	```
    24	
    25	**HiCache 曲线在控制器的整个运行区间（Rbar 1.25–1.73）内 eps 从未达到 1。**
    26	device-only 曲线有 2/4 段 eps>=1，HiCache 曲线 0/2 段。
    27	
    28	run 身份已核实：`pois200_pairB_cap2hc` / `pois200_cap3_hc` / `pois200_cap4_hc`，
    29	policy 分别为 static=2/3/4，pool 全部 101432（可比）。控制器车道脚本
    30	（`lane_var_foyer_g2.sh` 等）均设 `TURNSTILE_HICACHE_ARGS`，即控制器确实开 HiCache。
    31	
    32	## 后果一：§IV 现在的解释对控制器不成立
    33	
    34	论文现写：「Five sit at 1.63 or above, in the eps ≈ 1.15 segment where it
    35	does not pay at all.」—— 这句话把 device-only 的曲线套到 HiCache 的臂上。
    36	按控制器自己的配置，它们所在的段 eps 只有 0.19–0.46，**加并发仍然划算**。
    37	
    38	物理上也讲得通：device-only 的悬崖来自**重算**（§III 已测：五条 device-only run
    39	的 device miss 恰好等于重算 token，host 吸收为 0），而 HiCache 把驱逐的 KV 落到
    40	host 再读回，替代了重算，悬崖因此消失。τ 在同 Rbar 下的对比也支持：
    41	Rbar≈1.7 处 device tau=32.4–36.7，HiCache tau=29.8。
    42	
    43	## 后果二：19 分钟的那个反例，其实是 idle 不是步成本
    44	
    45	论文 sec_results.tex:156 用「hz1 与 cap3_hc 占用相同、Rbar 几乎相同，cap 却快 19 分钟」
    46	论证「低占用不能解释结果」，并把差额归给步成本。拆开 decom：
    47	
    48	```
    49	              Rbar_busy  idle_min  busy_min   wall_min
    50	cap3_hc         1.51       24.1      218.4     236.4
    51	foyer_hz1       1.52       36.7      225.2     255.8
    52	差                          +12.6      +6.8     +19.4
    53	```
    54	
    55	**65% 的差额（12.6/19.4）是 idle 时间**，不是步成本。两者步成本只差
    56	1.2 ms（29.2 vs 28.0，约 4%）。idle 变多与「控制器攥着 permit 不放」一致：
    57	会话一轮结束去 tool_wait 时，控制器不放进新的，引擎就空转。
    58	
    59	## 这对论文意味着什么
    60	
    61	现在稿子里有两条互相竞争的解释：
    62	  (a) 步成本悬崖（eps>=1）让加并发不划算
    63	  (b) 控制器高估占用 1.79 倍、只用到 49.2% 池子，不敢放
    64	
    65	新数据说 **(a) 在控制器实际配置下不存在**，所以 (b) 才是主因。
    66	这实际上是个**更好**的故事：判据的作用从「解释失败」变成「**排除掉工作负载侧的
    67	解释**，从而把失败定位在控制器自己身上」——这正是一个诊断工具该干的事，
    68	而且它不再需要跨配置外推。
    69	
    70	代价：摘要里「that is a property of the workload rather than of the controller」
    71	一句需要重写，§IV 的「operating region」一节需要重写。
    72	
    73	## 尚未确定 / 需要补的
    74	
    75	- HiCache 曲线只到 Rbar 1.73（没有 cap5_hc），**看不见更高处有没有悬崖**。
    76	  控制器区间（1.35–1.71）落在已测范围内，结论对该区间成立；对更高 Rbar 未知。
    77	- 每条 HiCache 静态 cap 只有一条 run（cap3_hc/cap4_hc 各有一条重复在跑/已有），
    78	  段弹性没有误差棒。
    79	- 控制器是否真的因 idle 而慢，需要在**全部**臂上验证 idle_pct 与 wall 的关系，
    80	  不能只看 hz1/cap3_hc 这一对。
    81	
    82	---
    83	
    84	# 追加发现（同日）：变负载 trace 上控制器投影饱和
    85	
    86	在 `pois200_var_foyer`（控制器主臂 `budget3:hw=0;target=0.95`，跑变负载 trace
    87	`replay_varload_lo2hi.csv`）上观测到准入停滞。核实过程：
    88	
    89	```
    90	waiting_n   = 22 ~ 23     有会话在等
    91	resident    = 0           引擎占用的 token 数（设备侧）
    92	usage       = 0.0
    93	valve       = False        SLO 阀门【没有】关
    94	gate_full   = False        round gate 没开（默认 round_gate=0）
    95	candidate   = None
    96	n_admitted  = 0
    97	```
    98	
    99	引擎侧同时是空的：metrics.csv 里 `num_running_reqs=0`、`num_used_tokens=0`、
   100	`num_queue_reqs=0`，`generation_tokens_total` 冻结在 136501。
   101	
   102	## 根因
   103	
   104	`controller_budget3.py:321-326`：
   105	
   106	```
   107	ctx_sum    = 72100     # 【在跑的 2 个会话】的上下文之和，各约 36000 token
   108	growth_sum = 26737
   109	base       = max(resident, ctx_sum) = 72100
   110	projection = base + growth_sum = 98837
   111	budget     = 96360
   112	```
   113	
   114	判定是 `if projection + n <= budget`（:406）。**projection 本身已经超过 budget，
   115	所以对任何候选者都不成立**，`candidates` 恒为空 → 一个都不放行。
   116	
   117	`ctx_sum` 中位 47164、最大 123312；`base` 最大 103469，均可达/超过 budget 96360。
   118	
   119	唯一在推进的是 starve guard（:413-419），它 bypass 预算直接强推 `waiting[0]`，
   120	但有 `--starve-cooldown`（默认 300 秒）限速。实测 admit 间隔中位 87 秒，
   121	最后几次 199 / 278 / 343 / 302 秒 —— 就是 cooldown 的节拍。70 分钟里放了 35 个。
   122	
   123	## 为什么这不是 bug，而是论文机制的极端形态
   124	
   125	投影把**停在 tool_wait 里的会话按完整上下文计费**，而它们的 KV 是可驱逐的、
   126	并不在引擎的 resident 计数里。这正是 §V「控制器认为快满了，其实空着一半」
   127	（投影 1.79 倍于真值）描述的同一个量，只是在这条 trace 上被推到了极端：
   128	**只要有两个大上下文会话同时停在 tool wait，控制器就完全停止接纳，而引擎空转。**
   129	
   130	论文 §V 的 Route 1 已经测过：把这个 double charge 去掉（`base=resident`）反而
   131	更差（慢 4.2 分钟、SLO −6.0pp）。所以控制器是按设计行事。
   132	
   133	## 对变负载实验的影响（待定，取决于最终能否跑完）
   134	
   135	- 按当前速率，200 个会话约需 6.7 小时，可能落在 12h 超时内。
   136	- 若跑完：这是一个**可用但极端**的数据点 —— 控制器在变负载下不是"输一点"，
   137	  而是退化成每 5 分钟放一个。
   138	- 若跑不完：按项目规矩记为部分 run，不与 999 步的臂同表比较。
   139	- **需要在论文里如实报告这个机制**，否则审稿人会把它当成隐藏的 bug。
   140	
   141	---
   142	
   143	# 追加发现（09-24 凌晨）：变负载切换基线第一次跑无效，根因是仪表坏了
   144	
   145	## 现象
   146	
   147	`pois200_var_switch` 跑完（999 步、250.9 分钟、rc=0），但 `switcher.jsonl` 只有
   148	**1 条**记录（t=0，cap=3），而 `--interval 2` 下 250 分钟本该写约 7500 条。
   149	
   150	## 排除
   151	
   152	`cap_switcher.py` 的日志是**条件写入**的：
   153	
   154	```python
   155	if nxt != cap or wrote is None:   # 只在 cap 变化时记
   156	```
   157	
   158	所以「只有 1 条」不等于「死了」，而是**一直在跑但从未切换**。
   159	
   160	## 根因
   161	
   162	```python
   163	EVENTS = ("queued",)     # 只数 queued 事件当到达
   164	```
   165	
   166	但 `static=N` 模式下 runner 立即放行、没有东西压着会话，
   167	`admissions.jsonl` 里 **400 条全是 admit 和 release，一个 queued 都没有**。
   168	于是 `n_recent` 恒为 0、`rate` 恒为 0、`hi_streak` 永远到不了 2，**全程一次没切**。
   169	
   170	**所以第一次的 `pois200_var_switch` 实际等价于一条静态 cap3 run**（cap 文件被写成
   171	3 后再没动过），仍可当 cap3 的数据点用，不必浪费。
   172	
   173	## 修法（改仪表，不改判据）
   174	
   175	第一次尝试改成数 `admit` 事件 —— **错了**，离线模拟显示它在 82 分钟就切、89 分钟
   176	又切回来，之后再不切。原因是 `admit` 衡量的是**放行速率**，被 cap=3 限住了，那是
   177	容量节流不是到达。
   178	
   179	最终改法：用 `admit` 事件的 `waited_ms` **反推真实到达时刻**
   180	（`arrival = ts - waited_ms/1000`）。这是可部署的量（一个会话等了多久），
   181	不是偷看 trace。验证：
   182	
   183	```
   184	反推到达的速率： 0-125 分 0.009~0.019/s  ← 低载相
   185	              125-175 分 0.030~0.037/s  ← 高载相
   186	切点 125 分，与 trace 设计的 124.5 分吻合。
   187	```
   188	
   189	离线重放（拿第一次 run 的 admissions.jsonl 走修好的逻辑）：**t=128.0 分切到 cap2**，
   190	滞后 3.5 分钟；之后保持 cap2。
   191	
   192	**切换规则本身（hi 0.025 / lo 0.020 / 窗 600s / 连续两个采样）一个字未改。**
   193	
   194	## 顺带纠正一处我对规则的误读
   195	
   196	`hi_streak >= 2` 数的是**连续两个 2 秒采样**（4 秒去抖），不是「两个 10 分钟窗」；
   197	600 秒窗只提供平滑。所以 codex 担心的「要 20 分钟才降档」，实测滞后 3.5 分钟。
   198	
   199	## 另一个观察：cap 在变负载 trace 上并不顶满引擎
   200	
   201	`pois200_var_switch`（cap=3）全程 `sglang:num_running_reqs` 中位 1~2、
   202	`sglang:num_queue_reqs` 中位 0（最大 2）—— **cap 从未被在跑数顶满**。
   203	cap 限的是**活跃会话数**（含停在 tool_wait 的），不是在跑数，
   204	所以引擎空着而会话在排队（积压最高 67 个）。这与子 agent 在 realhr 上的发现一致。
   205	
   206	---

   208	# 追加（09-24 03:00）：四条车道把卡 0/1 的两条 run 跑废了
   209	
   210	## 事实
   211	
   212	02:36 起，卡 0 的 `pois200_foyer_sf0` 与卡 1 的 `pois200_foyer_hc_r2` 双双失败：
   213	
   214	```
   215	                 尝试    成功    失败
   216	foyer_hc_r2       624     528     96     ← 未跑满 999 步
   217	foyer_sf0         679     595     84     ← 未跑满 999 步
   218	```
   219	
   220	失败模式：`steps.jsonl` 里 `status=FAILED`、错误为
   221	`request error: ... connection closed before message completed`，
   222	从 148.4 分钟起成簇出现，成功请求在 148.8 分钟处**全部停止**。
   223	server 日志末尾：`Subprocess scheduler_0 (pid=...) crashed with exit code -15`。
   224	
   225	两条 run 的引擎死在**同一绝对时刻**（02:36:46 与 02:37:00，相隔 14 秒），
   226	所以是外部杀死，不是自己崩的。
   227	
   228	## 根因：我违反了项目已经记下的教训
   229	
   230	`scripts/watchdog.sh` 的注释里写着（2026-09-21 学到）：
   231	
   232	> CARDS 0 AND 1 ARE DELIBERATELY ABSENT. ... because **saturating all four gets
   233	> our long-running processes reaped by other tenants on this shared box.**
   234	
   235	我把车道从 2 条加到 4 条，于是长跑进程被共享机器上其它租户的 reaper 杀掉。
   236	**SIGTERM 是结果不是原因** —— 我去查谁发的 SIGTERM 是白费功夫。
   237	
   238	## 处置
   239	
   240	1. 撤掉卡 0/1 的 stream 与 gate；总并发回到 <= 2。
   241	2. 三条失败的 run 目录挪到 `_failed_4lane_*`（按项目规矩，同名目录必须先挪走，
   242	   否则追加式日志会把两次 run 静默混合）。
   243	3. 重新串行排队：卡 2 跑 var_cap2 → foyer_hc_r2 → var_switch2；
   244	   卡 3 跑 var_cap3 → foyer_sf0_r2。lane 脚本的卡号/端口已相应改掉。
   245	4. 教训写进 `CLAUDE.md`「硬件与运维」节。
   246	
   247	## 一条仍然有效的观察
   248	
   249	`var_foyer`（变负载控制器臂）在四条车道期间**跑完了 999 步、0 失败**，
   250	所以它没有受影响。四条变负载臂里已完成的是 var_foyer 与 var_switch（后者等价于
   251	静态 cap3）。

    14	## 这个项目的三条血的教训
    15	
    16	1. **手算的表一定会错。** `paper/data/decomp.csv` 曾有一行 busy/idle 来自另一条 run、
    17	   wall 却是对的，两个数自相矛盾，图 2 因此画错，几个月没人发现。任何汇总表都应由
    18	   `scripts/lab/*.py` 生成并带断言（见 `gen_decomp.py`：busy+idle 必须精确等于采样跨度，
    19	   残差非零直接退出）。
    20	1b. **从错的列抄数 —— 已经犯过四次。** 手抄汇总表时把相邻一列的数当成目标列：
    21	   `pairB_cap2hc` 的 busy/idle 来自另一条 run、`foyer_hz2`/`foyer_hz0` 的 running
    22	   抄错、表 I 的 rg4/foyfix 两行 Δ 用了中途读数、相关工作的「round-1+ 命中率
    23	   89.8%」其实是 foyer_hc 的 **SLO**（真值 80.9%）。
    24	   **凡是从表里抄一个数，抄完回头看一眼它在原表里是哪一列。**
    25	
    26	2. **中位数会骗人。** `tool_wait` 中位 0.35 s 让整个项目以为会话不离开引擎；均值是 16.85 s。
    27	   报分布的时候，中位、均值、p90、最大值一起报。
    28	3. **"还没到" 和 "坏了" 长得一样。** 曾三次把「trace 的第一个会话还没到」误判成「实验卡住」。
    29	   判断一个 run 是否正常，先看 `runner.out` 和它已跑时长，别只看 steps=0。

    31	## 撤回记录

    35	`prompt_tokens_total == 计划 prefill` 反驳）、空转率随并发单调下降。

    58	  → `paper/data/concurrency.csv`）查明：同机同时 ≥4 条 run 且持续 ≥60 分钟，
    59	  会把**逐请求 decode 间隔中位 τ₂ 抬高 25~40%**（30.9~33.3 ms vs 同族干净值 23.8~25.7）。
    60	  2~3 条不抬（pairB_foyerhc 3 条跑 277 分钟，τ₂=23.8，正常）。
    61	  **危险之处：引擎侧所有指标都看不出异常** —— `num_running_reqs`、占用率、驱逐量、
    62	  重算量在这四条臂上与干净 run 不可区分，只有客户端实测的 τ₂ 露馅。
    63	  四条控制器臂（bres/hz0/hz2/breshz1）因此作废并重跑（新名加 `_r2`）。
    64	  详见 `docs/finding_concurrency_contamination_20260924.md`。
    65	  **判据要按时长不按峰值**：`foyer_hz1` 峰值到过 4 条但只有 14 分钟，τ₂ 正常。
    66	- **跑实验时必须挂 `gpu_watch.py`**（2026-09-24 用户提出，已接入 `run_matrix.py`）：
    67	  池子守卫只管【启动那一刻】，跑到一半别人往同一张卡上挂进程会静默污染这一段的
    68	  tau / SLO / 墙钟，而数据看上去完全正常。watcher 每 15 秒记一次该卡的显存、
    69	  利用率、全部计算进程及其属主，输出到 `<run_dir>/gpu_watch.jsonl`；属主不是自己的
    70	  标成 foreign。汇总用 `gpu_watch_summary.py <rundir>`，有它就可以写"这次测量期间
    71	  该卡独占"。**这是低概率情况的便宜保险，不是对已有结果的质疑**：起跑时的池子守卫
    72	  已保证池子严格对齐，且多次重复之间墙钟 CV 只有 0.9~1.8% —— 真被偷算力不会这么稳。
    73	  所以早期 run 照常可信，不因缺这个记录而作废。
    74	- KV pool 必须落在 `101,432 ± 20`，否则该 run 作废，不记录——SGLang 按启动时的空闲显存
    75	  定池子，池子小了数字就不可比。

    77	## 改文件

    79	- **`open(path,"w")` 会在 write 之前就截断文件。** 2026-09-24 犯过：`s.replace(...)`
    80	  后面多打一个逗号使返回值成了元组，write 抛 TypeError，但文件已经被清空

   133	
   134	\subsection{The controllers leave memory unused}
   135	\label{sec:results:idle-memory}
   136	
   137	Before the routes, one measurement that reframes all of them. Our controller
   138	keeps every session's permit for its lifetime, so its pool occupancy is a
   139	policy output, not a constraint. It runs at a median of 49.2\% of the pool on
   140	the base configuration, and the median number of sessions queueing for
   145	limitation these works would press on.
   146	
   147	\paragraph{Prefix caching.} The prefix cache is often proposed as the signal
   148	an admission policy should use. In our workload it is not a policy variable:
   149	round-1+ hit rate is a function of the admission policy, and of the host tier,
   150	in a way an earlier draft of this paper got wrong in both directions. Without
   151	HiCache it collapses as the cap rises: 80.8\%, 78.5\%, 70.6\%, 56.0\%, 32.8\%
   152	for caps of one through five, against a planned 80.9\% for the trace. With
   153	HiCache it does not: 80.7\%, 79.6\%, 78.7\% for caps of two, three and four.
   154	Our controller sits at 80.6\%.
   155	So the honest statement is not that admission does not move this quantity---%
   156	\texttt{cap4} alone moves it 25 points---but that the host tier is what keeps
   157	it from moving, which is the same mechanism \S\ref{sec:model} and
   158	\S\ref{sec:criterion} keep running into. A configuration without a host tier
   159	that admits four sessions does not have the cache that a host-tier one at the
   160	same concurrency does. (Both numbers are the
   161	unweighted mean of the per-step planned hit rate over rounds 1 and later; the
   162	token-weighted figure for the same steps is 86.2\%, and the all-steps figure
   163	is 73.8\%. We use the unweighted round-1+ convention throughout, and flag it
   164	because the paper's earlier drafts mixed conventions.) We note the boundary of this claim---we varied admission policy,
   165	not eviction policy, and SGLang exposes alternate eviction policies we did not
   165	\subsection{What we do not claim}
   166	
   167	We do not claim that admission control is useless in general. We claim it does
   168	not pay \emph{here}, we show the measurement that decides it, and we argue
   169	(\S\ref{sec:criterion}) that the same measurement can be taken on another
   170	workload before committing to a sweep. We also do not claim our hardware and model are
   171	representative; our results are from one 24\,GB accelerator and one 7B model,
   172	and extending them is future work.
    55	
    56	We are explicit about what this does and does not establish. The HiCache curve
    57	rests on three configurations and two segments, one run each, with no
    58	error bars, and it stops at 1.73---we cannot see whether a cliff appears
    59	beyond the controllers' region. A $\pm10\%$ perturbation of $\tau$ puts the
    60	segment elasticities' bounds across one even on the device-only curve, so the
    61	cliff is a description of the fitted points rather than a threshold we have
    62	located precisely. And it is measured on
    63	different policies rather than by an intervention, so it is a
    64	cross-policy association. On our data it is also computed from the same wall
    65	clock the model then explains---$\tau_1$ is derived from $W_{\text{busy}}$, so
    66	using it to predict $W_{\text{busy}}$ restates rather than tests---and its
    67	points are different policies, so the slope is a cross-policy association, not
    68	a transportable causal derivative. $\tau_2$ is genuinely independent of the
    69	wall clock but measures only decode, so it cannot be dropped into a throughput
    70	equation that includes prefill.
    71	
    72	We tested whether the curve transfers to a second workload and it does not
    73	obviously do so. On a real agent trace (117 sessions, tool waits of seconds
    74	rather than the 95\,ms of the synthetic trace, three static caps and one
    75	controller arm), the model-consistent elasticity is negative across the measured
    76	range---$-2.40$ then $-0.28$, at $\overline{R}_{\text{busy}}$ 1.08 to 1.29---and
    77	the controller arm there runs at 1.22, below every one of the nine arms above,
    78	and still does not beat the static front. The per-request measure has the
    79	opposite sign on that trace ($+0.41$, $+0.48$). We report this as a boundary:
    80	the criterion is a description of one workload on one configuration, and where
    81	we could check it elsewhere it neither transferred nor explained the failure.
    82	It is not, as an earlier draft claimed, something that decides the question in
    83	minutes before building anything.

    42	def main():
    43	    ev_p, ar_p, dc_p, out_p = sys.argv[1:5]
    44	    ev, ar, dc = load(ev_p), load(ar_p), load(dc_p)
    45	    # 争用标记：被主机争用抬高了 tau 的 run 不能拿 tau 列当证据。
    46	    # 复用列（reuse_*/recompute_M）是【体积比】，与时间无关，不受争用影响 ——
    47	    # 实测这四条臂的 reuse 与同族干净 run 一致（0.691~0.694），只有 tau 被抬高。
    48	    conc = {}
    49	    if len(sys.argv) > 5 and os.path.exists(sys.argv[5]):
    50	        # 注意去掉 pois200_ 前缀再配对：eviction.csv 用的是短名（cap1 / foyer_hz2），
    51	        # concurrency.csv 用的是 run 目录名（pois200_cap1 / pois200_foyer_hz2）。
    52	        # 不归一化会静默全部落空，contested 列全变成 "?"（2026-09-24 犯过一次）。
    53	        conc = {r["run"].replace("pois200_", ""): r["verdict"] for r in
    54	                csv.DictReader(l for l in open(sys.argv[5]) if not l.startswith("#"))}
    55	        assert len(conc) > 50, "concurrency.csv 只读到 %d 行，路径或格式不对" % len(conc)
    56	
    57	    rows, bad = [], []
    58	    for run, e in ev.items():
    59	        a, d = ar.get(run), dc.get(run)
    60	        if not a or not d:
    61	            bad.append((run, "arm_running 或 decomp 里没有这条"))
    62	            continue
    63	        prompt = float(e["prompt_M"])
    64	        dev_miss = float(e["dev_miss_M"])
    65	        host_hit = float(e["host_hit_M"])
    66	        unc = float(e["uncached_M"])
    67	        reuse_dev = prompt - dev_miss
    68	        reuse_tot = reuse_dev + host_hit
    69	
    70	        # 断言 1：设备未命中 = host 接住 + 真正重算。这是这套记账的定义式，
    71	        # 挡住的是从 eviction.csv 抄错列（抄错不会有这个性质）。
    72	        assert abs(dev_miss - (host_hit + unc)) < 0.15, \
    73	            "%s: dev_miss %.1f != host_hit %.1f + uncached %.1f" % (run, dev_miss, host_hit, unc)
    74	        # 断言 2：复用 + 重算 = 总 prompt
    75	        assert abs(reuse_tot + unc - prompt) < 0.15, \
    76	            "%s: reuse %.1f + uncached %.1f != prompt %.1f" % (run, reuse_tot, unc, prompt)
    77	
    78	        rows.append({
    79	            "run": run,
    80	            "family": "hicache" if e["hicache"] == "1" else "device_only",
    81	            "Rbar_busy": a["running_busy"],
    82	            "prompt_M": "%.1f" % prompt,
    83	            "reuse_dev_M": "%.1f" % reuse_dev,
    84	            "reuse_host_M": "%.1f" % host_hit,
    85	            "reuse_total_M": "%.1f" % reuse_tot,
    86	            "reuse_frac": "%.3f" % (reuse_tot / prompt),
    87	            "recompute_M": "%.1f" % unc,
    88	            "tau1_ms": d["step_back_ms"],
    89	            "contested": conc.get(run, "?"),
    90	        })
    91	
    92	    rows.sort(key=lambda r: (r["family"], float(r["Rbar_busy"])))
    93	    header = "\n".join([
    94	        "# 前缀复用的天花板 vs 内存梯队 —— 由 scripts/lab/gen_reuse.py 生成，勿手改",
    95	        "# reuse_dev_M   = prompt - dev_miss       设备侧 radix cache 兑现的复用",
    96	        "# reuse_host_M  = host 梯队接住的（device-only 恒为 0）",
    97	        "# reuse_total_M = 两者之和 = 真正没有重算的那部分 prompt",
    98	        "# reuse_frac    = reuse_total / prompt —— device-only 随 R̄ 塌，hicache 守住",
    99	        "# 天花板（trace 允许的最大复用）≈ 19.9 M；兑现它与否是【配置】的属性",
   100	        "# contested: 该 run 是否落在主机争用窗口里（见 concurrency.csv）。CONTESTED 的",
   101	        "#            tau1_ms 列不可用；reuse_* 是体积比，与时间无关，不受争用影响。",
   102	        "run,family,Rbar_busy,prompt_M,reuse_dev_M,reuse_host_M,reuse_total_M,reuse_frac,recompute_M,tau1_ms,contested"])
   103	    fields = ["run", "family", "Rbar_busy", "prompt_M", "reuse_dev_M", "reuse_host_M",
   104	              "reuse_total_M", "reuse_frac", "recompute_M", "tau1_ms", "contested"]
   105	    body = header + "\n" + "\n".join(",".join(str(r[k]) for k in fields) for r in rows) + "\n"
   106	    assert isinstance(body, str)
   107	    with open(out_p, "w") as fh:
   108	        fh.write(body)
   109	
   110	    print("写入 %s：%d 行" % (out_p, len(rows)))
   111	    for r in rows:
   112	        print("  %-16s %-11s R̄ %-6s 复用 %5s M (%.3f)  重算 %5s M  τ₁ %-6s %s" % (
   113	            r["run"], r["family"], r["Rbar_busy"], r["reuse_total_M"],
   114	            float(r["reuse_frac"]), r["recompute_M"], r["tau1_ms"],
   115	            "<<CONTESTED" if r["contested"] == "CONTESTED" else ""))
   116	    if bad:
   117	        print("!! 未写入：")
   118	        for n, why in bad:
   119	            print("   %-16s %s" % (n, why))
   120	
   121	
   122	if __name__ == "__main__":
   123	    main()
     1	# 池占用与排队 —— 由 scripts/lab/gen_occupancy.py 生成

     1	# Foyer — 项目约定
     2	
     3	内存受限 GPU 上 agentic LLM serving 的准入控制研究。论文在 `paper/`（IEEEtran + LaTeX），
     4	实验在共享的 2×RTX 3090 上跑，原始日志在实验机 `lab-3090:/data/xbw/turnstile/results/`。
     5	
     6	## 证据纪律（所有工作都适用）
     7	
     8	- **不许编造**引用、实验数字、数据集、结果。数字对不上就报对不上，不要顺手改圆。
     9	- **论文里出现的每个数字都要能追到**代码、日志或 `paper/data/*.csv`。追不到的就标出来，
    10	  不要留在正文里。
    11	- **改 LaTeX 不许改科学含义**。措辞归措辞，结论归结论，两者分开说。
    12	- 下结论前先看文件。涉及实验的陈述必须来自 `results/`、`paper/data/` 或实验机日志。
    13	
    14	## 这个项目的三条血的教训
    15	
    16	1. **手算的表一定会错。** `paper/data/decomp.csv` 曾有一行 busy/idle 来自另一条 run、
    17	   wall 却是对的，两个数自相矛盾，图 2 因此画错，几个月没人发现。任何汇总表都应由
    18	   `scripts/lab/*.py` 生成并带断言（见 `gen_decomp.py`：busy+idle 必须精确等于采样跨度，
    19	   残差非零直接退出）。
    20	1b. **从错的列抄数 —— 已经犯过四次。** 手抄汇总表时把相邻一列的数当成目标列：
    21	   `pairB_cap2hc` 的 busy/idle 来自另一条 run、`foyer_hz2`/`foyer_hz0` 的 running
    22	   抄错、表 I 的 rg4/foyfix 两行 Δ 用了中途读数、相关工作的「round-1+ 命中率
    23	   89.8%」其实是 foyer_hc 的 **SLO**（真值 80.9%）。
    24	   **凡是从表里抄一个数，抄完回头看一眼它在原表里是哪一列。**
    25	
    26	2. **中位数会骗人。** `tool_wait` 中位 0.35 s 让整个项目以为会话不离开引擎；均值是 16.85 s。
    27	   报分布的时候，中位、均值、p90、最大值一起报。
    28	3. **"还没到" 和 "坏了" 长得一样。** 曾三次把「trace 的第一个会话还没到」误判成「实验卡住」。
    29	   判断一个 run 是否正常，先看 `runner.out` 和它已跑时长，别只看 steps=0。
    30	
    31	## 撤回记录
    32	
    33	已经撤回的主张不许复活，除非有新证据：`tool_wait` 0.1s→16.85s、oracle 单调性、
    34	记账重复计费、驱逐→重算推高步时间（被 `num_retracted_reqs≈0` 与
    35	`prompt_tokens_total == 计划 prefill` 反驳）、空转率随并发单调下降。
    36	详见 `paper/claims_audit.md` 与 `docs/claim_register_20260921.md`。
    37	
    38	## 引用红线
    39	
    40	- arXiv **2608.30830** 自报对 MORI/Continuum 的 head-to-head 胜利**已被否证，禁引**；
    41	  它的 41% goodput 损失与 ~10× 复活差可以引。
    42	- **PrefixShield (2608.01657)** 已占 "admission responsibility" 术语，需避撞。
    43	- 任何引用的标题、作者、年份都必须核实过才能进 `refs.bib`。**不要凭记忆写文献。**
    44	
    45	## 硬件与运维
    46	
    47	- 平时只用**卡 2 和卡 3**。卡 0/1 仅限 00:00–08:00 且确认空闲（`memory.used < 200 MiB`）。
    48	- **别人占着卡就等，不要算剩余空间够不够。只在完整的卡上跑。**
    49	- **不要同时占满四张卡。** 卡 0/1 可用时，总并发仍应 <= 2 条车道。
    50	  2026-09-21 学到过一次（记在 `scripts/watchdog.sh` 注释里）：把四张卡全占满，
    51	  长跑进程会被共享机器上其它租户的 reaper 杀掉。**2026-09-24 又犯了一次**：
    52	  四条车道一起开，卡 0/1 的两条 run 在 02:36:46 / 02:37:00（相隔 14 秒，
    53	  同一绝对时刻）双双被 SIGTERM，只跑到 679 和 624 步就终止，两条都作废。
    54	  症状：`steps.jsonl` 里 `connection closed before message completed` 在某个时刻
    55	  突然成簇出现，`status=FAILED`；server 日志里 `Subprocess scheduler_0 crashed
    56	  with exit code -15`。**这个 SIGTERM 是结果不是原因，别去查谁发的。**
    57	- **2026-09-24 追加：四条车道就算没被杀，数据也已经废了。** 审计（`scripts/lab/gen_concurrency.py`
    58	  → `paper/data/concurrency.csv`）查明：同机同时 ≥4 条 run 且持续 ≥60 分钟，
    59	  会把**逐请求 decode 间隔中位 τ₂ 抬高 25~40%**（30.9~33.3 ms vs 同族干净值 23.8~25.7）。
    60	  2~3 条不抬（pairB_foyerhc 3 条跑 277 分钟，τ₂=23.8，正常）。
    61	  **危险之处：引擎侧所有指标都看不出异常** —— `num_running_reqs`、占用率、驱逐量、
    62	  重算量在这四条臂上与干净 run 不可区分，只有客户端实测的 τ₂ 露馅。
    63	  四条控制器臂（bres/hz0/hz2/breshz1）因此作废并重跑（新名加 `_r2`）。
    64	  详见 `docs/finding_concurrency_contamination_20260924.md`。
    65	  **判据要按时长不按峰值**：`foyer_hz1` 峰值到过 4 条但只有 14 分钟，τ₂ 正常。
    66	- **跑实验时必须挂 `gpu_watch.py`**（2026-09-24 用户提出，已接入 `run_matrix.py`）：
    67	  池子守卫只管【启动那一刻】，跑到一半别人往同一张卡上挂进程会静默污染这一段的
    68	  tau / SLO / 墙钟，而数据看上去完全正常。watcher 每 15 秒记一次该卡的显存、
    69	  利用率、全部计算进程及其属主，输出到 `<run_dir>/gpu_watch.jsonl`；属主不是自己的
    70	  标成 foreign。汇总用 `gpu_watch_summary.py <rundir>`，有它就可以写"这次测量期间
    71	  该卡独占"。**这是低概率情况的便宜保险，不是对已有结果的质疑**：起跑时的池子守卫
    72	  已保证池子严格对齐，且多次重复之间墙钟 CV 只有 0.9~1.8% —— 真被偷算力不会这么稳。
    73	  所以早期 run 照常可信，不因缺这个记录而作废。
    74	- KV pool 必须落在 `101,432 ± 20`，否则该 run 作废，不记录——SGLang 按启动时的空闲显存
    75	  定池子，池子小了数字就不可比。

    77	## 改文件
    78	
    79	- **`open(path,"w")` 会在 write 之前就截断文件。** 2026-09-24 犯过：`s.replace(...)`
    80	  后面多打一个逗号使返回值成了元组，write 抛 TypeError，但文件已经被清空

    83	  征兆是**页数突然变少** —— 编译后页数掉了，先怀疑有文件被清空。
    84	- 长文档改完后 `git diff --stat` 看一眼增删行数，与预期差太多就停下查。

The main reuse percentages trace to `reuse.csv`, but the stated 19.9M “workload ceiling” appears to be the maximum observed reuse, not an independently computed trace bound. I’m checking that distinction and the frontier text, where one repeat appears to be described incorrectly.

 succeeded in 0ms:
    35	5.1\,pp (\texttt{replicates.csv}). The two widest belong to the two arms that
    36	decide something---\texttt{horizon}=1 and the leftmost anchor---so where a small
    37	margin matters our repeats are weakest, and where they are strongest (the base
    38	configuration and the \texttt{cap3}+HiCache anchor) the margin is not small. We
    39	report both ends rather than the average.}
    40	
    41	The anchor repeats moved the frontier twice and we report both moves. The
    42	leftmost anchor's second run came out both faster (209.1 against 213.0 minutes)
    43	and higher on SLO (68.5 against 63.4), displacing the first; no controller
    44	verdict moves, because every controller finishes to the right of the next
    45	anchor. The middle anchor's repeat moved it the other way for us: it is also
    46	faster and higher, and taking it moves our best arm from \emph{on} the line to
    47	\emph{on the line, below it}. We report every repeat we ran rather than keeping
    48	the better of each pair, because a frontier whose anchors are single
    49	measurements is the weakness this whole subsection is about---and here it is
    50	the weakness biting.
    51	
    52	The other end of the front deserves its own sentence, because the rest of this
    53	paper is negative and a reader could close it thinking the answer is to do
    54	nothing. The run with \emph{no} admission control---every session admitted the
    55	moment it arrives---finishes in 288.6 minutes at 3.0\% SLO, $87.6$\,pp below
    56	the envelope and the worst SLO we report. Its median
    57	time-to-first-token is 1{,}774 seconds, its prefix cache hit rate collapses from
    58	the planned 75.5\% the runner prints in its end-of-run summary (the all-steps
    59	convention, a different
    60	one from the round-1+ numbers in \S\ref{sec:related}---for the same trace
    61	\texttt{gen\_table.py} reports 73.8\% on that convention) to 1.6\%, and its segment SLO is 30\% in the first
    62	tenth of the run and exactly zero in each of the nine after that. Uncontrolled
    63	admission does not degrade gracefully; it destroys the cache and then serves
    64	almost nobody. The question this paper addresses is therefore not whether to
    65	admit selectively, but which selective rule to use.
    66	
    67	Two features of the front are worth noting before the negative results.
    68	The envelope is steep between 209 and 230 minutes (0.63\,pp per minute) and
    69	shallow thereafter (0.15\,pp per minute), so the cost of buying SLO by
    70	retreating from the fast end is high. And $W$ is not monotone in concurrency:
    71	five sessions (260.9\,min) is \emph{slower} than four (249.9\,min)---the same
    72	configuration family, no host cache on either side---because the per-step cost
    73	rises faster than the request count does. We return to this
    74	non-monotonicity in \S\ref{sec:model}, where it turns out to be the first
    75	appearance of the mechanism that drives the whole frontier.
    76	
    77	\begin{table}[tb]
    78	  \caption{All completed runs. \emph{Conc.} is the time-weighted mean number
    79	  of \emph{live} sessions, which is not the running-request count $\overline{R}$
    80	  used in \S\ref{sec:model}---a live session is parked in a tool wait most of
   270	\end{center}
   271	
   272	Four of the eleven are attributed. Seven are not: their occupancy and their step
   273	cost both sit close to their comparison cap's, and their shortfall is real but
   274	we cannot say from these measurements which mechanism produced it. We publish
   275	the table rather than a summary because a summary would claim more than we
   276	have---and because ``two mechanisms explain the negative result'' would be the
   277	wrong summary. Two mechanisms explain part of it, on four of the eleven arms;
   278	the rest is a measurement we can make and cannot yet interpret.
   279	
   280	\subsection{Route 1: the accounting base}
   281	
   282	Our controller's projection (Eq.~\ref{eq:proj}) charges a session for its full
   283	context even while it is parked in a tool wait, when its KV is evictable and
   284	therefore absent from the engine's reported occupancy. We argued this was a
   285	double charge and removed it, admitting on engine telemetry alone.
   286	
   287	The charge is not double. Removing it did admit more---median engine occupancy
   288	rose from 49.2\% to 56.9\% of the pool---and the round-1+ prefix hit rate held at
   289	80.2\% against the 80.6\% of the run that keeps the charge, so no eviction spiral occurred. But the run was
   290	4.2 minutes \emph{slower} and lost 6.0\,pp of SLO. Per \S\ref{sec:model}, the
   291	extra occupancy raised both $\overline{R}$ (1.35 to 1.48) and $\tau_2$
   292	(23.8\,ms to 31.3\,ms), and the two cancelled.
   293	
   294	We note that this claim had already been falsified once by source inspection:
   295	SGLang's \texttt{token\_usage} subtracts evictable blocks, so a parked
   296	session's prefix is genuinely not counted, and charging for it reserves
   297	capacity that really will be needed back. The end-to-end measurement agrees
   298	with the source reading.
   299	
   300	\subsection{Route 2: the growth reserve}
   301	
   302	Sessions grow by roughly 2.5K tokens per round, so the controller reserves
   303	capacity for the next \texttt{horizon} rounds. We swept the horizon over
   304	$\{0,1,2,3\}$ and the reserve is not a free parameter to be minimised:
   305	Fig.~\ref{fig:horizon} shows the response is non-monotone. Removing the
   306	reserve entirely costs 12.6\,pp of SLO, and a reserve of two rounds is
   307	15 minutes \emph{slower} than three. The optimum is at one round, and there it comes closest to the
   308	static front---0.92\,pp below it, inside the tie band---and that single point is
   309	the one this paper spends the most words on, because it does not reproduce: the
   310	repeat of this configuration lands 5.6\,pp below the line
   311	(\S\ref{sec:results}). Read the sweep as a shape, not as a result at its
   312	minimum.
   313	
   314	\subsection{Route 3: the hard ceiling}
   315	
   316	Lowering the budget fraction is the crudest available knob and behaves as the
   317	other routes do: it slides the operating point along the front. We report it
   318	only for completeness, since it adds no mechanism.
   319	
   320	\subsection{An independent adaptive controller}
   321	\label{sec:results:aimd}
   322	
   323	Every controller above is ours, which invites the objection that the negative
   324	result is a statement about one design rather than about adaptive admission.
   215	
   216	Two readings, and we give both. The first is that our headline claim is
   217	conditional: at the strict threshold that interactive serving actually cares
   218	about, no controller reaches the static front; at a loose one, one does. The
   219	second is that the crossing is still not a win, though the reason is more
   220	awkward than we first wrote. $+2.7$\,pp at 20\,s is larger than the replicate
   221	SLO spread of two of the three controller-side configurations we repeated
   222	(1.0 and 0.5\,pp) but smaller than the best arm's own (3.9\,pp) and smaller
   223	than an envelope anchor's (5.1\,pp)---and it is the
   224	anchors, not the controllers, that set the line the margin is measured
   225	against. A third pair
   226	exists---two runs of \texttt{cap3}+HiCache---but one of them exceeded its own
   227	cap, so we do not use it as a replicate. A margin that appears only at one threshold
   228	and only at a size we cannot resolve is not evidence that adaptive admission
   229	works; it is evidence that the question needs more runs than we have. We state
   230	the conditional version in the abstract, and we flag this as the second thing
   231	we would re-run with more GPU time.
   270	therefore rests on the original anchors without their repeats, and a
   271	ready-clock frontier that included them could move the line by the 3.9 and
   272	5.1\,pp those repeats differ by. We do not claim the verdict is unchanged under
   273	every SLO definition---only that on the runs we could recompute, moving the
   274	clock to the ready point costs every configuration 3--19\,pp and changes no
   275	verdict in that set.
   276	We report this because a reviewer should ask it,
   277	and because the answer is the useful part: the negative result is not an
   278	artifact of choosing an SLO definition that flatters controllers. If anything
   279	the flattering choice was the one that moved our best arm's distance from
   280	$-0.92$ to $+0.22$---that is, from just below the line to just above it, with
   281	neither number outside the tie band.
   282	
   283	\paragraph{Limitations.} Our results come from one 24\,GB accelerator, one 7B
   284	model and one trace family; generality is untested. Only four configurations
   285	have been repeated---twice, twice, twice and three times---so the replicate
   286	spread we report in \S\ref{sec:results} is estimated from one to two degrees of
   287	freedom per configuration; a margin as small as the one that decides our verdict would need several
   288	more repeats per configuration to resolve, and we did not run them. Two specific boundaries
   289	matter. First, the pool holds about four median sessions, so concurrency is
   290	capacity-bound by construction; on a device with a much larger pool the

     1	\section{Results: Four Routes, One Outcome}
     2	\label{sec:results}
     3	
     4	\subsection{The frontier}
     5	
     6	Fig.~\ref{fig:frontier} places every completed run on the (wall clock, SLO)
     7	plane and draws the upper-left envelope of the static points. Table~\ref{tab:runs}
     8	gives the numbers and each controller's signed distance from that envelope.
     9	
    10	The result is unambiguous. Of nine controller variants, \emph{none} is above
    11	the envelope---not one, on any run, and the pair we repeated most tightly says
    12	so at a resolution the others do not. The closest arm of all is the one with
    13	single-flight admission disabled, at $0.55$ percentage points \emph{below} the
    14	line---inside our $\pm1.0$\,pp tie band, so we record it as \emph{on} the
    15	line rather than below it, but on the below side of it.
    16	
    17	The base configuration is the one whose repeats we can trust, and they are
    18	tight: four runs spanning 0.5\,pp of SLO (89.5\% mean, 279.8 to 293.2 minutes),
    19	against the 3.3\,pp that separates it from the single-flight-off arm. So that
    20	difference is several times the noise of its own configuration rather than
    21	inside it, and it is a trade rather than a win: 28 minutes of wall clock bought
    22	with 3.5\,pp of SLO, moving the arm onto the tie band from below it. Not one
    23	configuration in the table gets above the line, and the tightest pair we have
    24	is what makes us willing to say the near misses are misses. A fifth pair, two runs of \texttt{cap3}+HiCache, \emph{is} used, and an
    25	earlier draft of this paper wrongly set it aside saying one of them exceeded
    26	its own cap. That was a different run: the over-cap one is the device-only
    27	\texttt{cap3} repeat (peak in-flight five against a cap of three, 126
    28	over-cap samples), while both HiCache runs peak at three with none. The
    29	repeat is also both faster and higher on SLO (230.3 minutes, 81.9\% against
    30	236.4 and 81.1\%), so by the same rule that displaced \texttt{cap4}+HiCache's
    31	first run it displaces this one---and moving that anchor is what puts our
    32	best arm below the line rather than on it.\footnote{The repeats are uneven, and
    33	we do not want the tightest pair to stand for all of them. Five configurations
    80	configuration is 1.79$\times$ the truth (1.0--4.1$\times$ across all of them)---and the per-step elasticity measured on the
    81	configuration they actually run stays below one across their whole operating
    82	range, so the engine had room they did not take: the ceiling we measure on
    83	device-only caps does not survive on the host-tier configuration the
    84	controllers use. The wall
    85	clock splits into busy and idle time; admission policy moves only the running
    86	count, and $\tau$ rises with it. The idle component (7.4--36.3\% of the
    87	sampled span) comes from gaps the tool waits set---an agent session issues no
    88	request while a tool runs,
    89	and that wait averages 16.85\,s, not the sub-second figure the trace's median
    90	suggests---though how many such gaps a run collects is partly the policy's,
    91	since a policy sets how many sessions stay live. On device-only configurations we can account for the rise token for
    92	token; on host-tier ones we cannot, and we say so. We also report the six of
    93	our own claims we retracted along the way, and the boundary of the verdict: at
    94	a loose latency threshold one variant does cross the front, by a margin we
    95	cannot resolve against run-to-run noise.
    96	\end{abstract}

     3

    26	    def wt(rows):
    27	        num = sum(s["planned_prefix_hit_rate"] * (s.get("prefix_len") or 0) for s in rows)
    28	        den = sum((s.get("prefix_len") or 0) for s in rows)
    29	        return 100 * num / den if den else None
    30	    a, r1 = sel(st), sel([s for s in st if s["round_idx"] >= 1])
    31	    return {"plan_all_mean": aw(a), "plan_all_tok": wt(a),
    32	            "plan_r1_mean": aw(r1), "plan_r1_tok": wt(r1)}

    34	
    35	def main():
    36	    root, out = sys.argv[1], sys.argv[2]
    37	    rows = []
    38	    print("%-10s %10s %10s %10s %10s" % ("run", "TTFT中位s", "TTFT p90s", "SLO%", "计划命中(r1均值)%"))
    39	    for r in RUNS:
    40	        p = os.path.join(root, "pois200_" + r, "steps.jsonl")
    41	        if not os.path.exists(p):
    42	            continue
    43	        st = [json.loads(l) for l in open(p) if l.strip()]
    44	        tt = sorted(s["first_token_ms"] for s in st if s.get("first_token_ms") is not None)
    45	        if not tt:
    46	            continue
    47	        h = hit(r, root)
    48	        rows.append({
    49	            "run": r, "n_steps": len(st),
    50	            "ttft_p50_s": round(tt[len(tt) // 2] / 1000, 1),
    51	            "ttft_p90_s": round(tt[int(.9 * len(tt))] / 1000, 1),
    52	            "slo_pct": round(100 * sum(1 for x in tt if x < 10000) / len(tt), 1),
    53	            **{k: (round(v, 2) if v else "") for k, v in h.items()},
    54	        })
    55	        print("%-10s %10.1f %10.1f %10.1f %10.1f" % (
    56	            r, rows[-1]["ttft_p50_s"], rows[-1]["ttft_p90_s"], rows[-1]["slo_pct"],
    57	            h.get("plan_r1_mean") or 0))
    58	    with open(out, "w", newline="") as f:
    59	        f.write("# 无准入控制基线 + 命中率的四种口径 —— 由 scripts/lab/gen_nocontrol.py 生成\n")
    60	        f.write("# ttft_p50_s/p90_s: 引擎侧 first_token_ms 的分位（不含等 permit）\n")
    61	        f.write("# plan_*: 四种口径。论文 §Related Work 用 plan_r1_mean；\n")
    62	        f.write("#         无控制那段的 75.5% 是 runner 自己报的全步口径，与此不同\n")
    63	        w = csv.DictWriter(f, fieldnames=list(rows[0].keys()))
    64	        w.writeheader(); w.writerows(rows)
    65	    print("->", out)
    60	        c = line.rstrip("\n").split(",")
    61	        if len(c) <= i:
    62	            continue
    63	        try:
    64	            out.append(float(c[i]))
    65	        except ValueError:
    66	            pass
    67	    return out

    70	def main():
    71	    root, out_path = sys.argv[1], sys.argv[2]
    72	    runs = sys.argv[3:] or RUNS
    73	    rows, bad = [], []
    74	    for r in runs:
    75	        d = os.path.join(root, "pois200_" + r)
    76	        mp = os.path.join(d, "metrics.csv")
    77	        sp = os.path.join(d, "steps.jsonl")
    78	        if not (os.path.exists(mp) and os.path.exists(sp)):
    79	            bad.append((r, "缺 metrics.csv 或 steps.jsonl"))
    80	            continue
    81	        try:
    82	            prompt = last_value(mp, "sglang:prompt_tokens_total")
    83	            cached = last_value(mp, "sglang:cached_tokens_total")
    84	            evicted = last_value(mp, "sglang:evicted_tokens_total")
    85	            steps = [json.loads(l) for l in open(sp) if l.strip()]
    86	            uncached = sum(s.get("server_uncached_prompt_tokens") or 0 for s in steps)
    87	            plan = sum(s.get("prompt_len") or 0 for s in steps)
    88	
    89	            # 断言 1：prompt_tokens_total 必须等于计划 prefill —— 这是恒等式，
    90	            # 挡住的是列读错（读错列不会有这个性质）
    91	            assert prompt and abs(prompt - plan) / plan < 0.01, \
    92	                "prompt_tokens_total %.1fM 与计划 %.1fM 不符，列可能读错了" % (
    93	                    (prompt or 0) / 1e6, plan / 1e6)
    94	
    95	            # 断言 2：cached 必须是 prompt 的一部分（不是独立的量）
    96	            assert cached is not None and 0 <= cached <= prompt * 1.01, \
    97	                "cached_tokens_total 不在 [0, prompt] 内"
    98	            # 注意：prompt − cached == steps 的未命中数，这条【只在无 HiCache 时成立】。
    99	            # 有 HiCache 时两者差很多（cap4_hc: 22.4M vs 9.1M），因为
   100	            # sglang:cached_tokens_total 只计【设备侧】radix cache 的命中，
   101	            # host 梯队接住的那部分不进这个计数器，但逐请求 usage 里算作 cached。
   102	            # 所以本表以 steps 的未命中数为准（那才是真正重新 prefill 的量），
   103	            # 并把设备侧命中单列出来，两者的差就是 host 梯队的贡献。
   104	            dev_miss = prompt - cached   # 设备侧未命中
   105	
   106	            rn = col(mp, "sglang:num_running_reqs")
   107	            # metadata.json 里没有 hicache_enabled 这个键（核过），所以按已知
   108	            # 配置判定：HiCache 只在这些 run 上开。这比猜 metadata 的键名可靠。
   109	            HC = {"pairB_cap2hc", "cap3_hc", "cap4_hc", "foyer_hc",
   110	                  "foyer_hz1", "foyer_hz2", "foyer_hz0", "foyer_bres"}
   111	            rows.append({
   112	                "run": r,
   113	                "hicache": "1" if r in HC else "0",
   114	                "prompt_M": round(prompt / 1e6, 1),
   115	
   116	                "uncached_M": round(uncached / 1e6, 1),
   117	                "evicted_M": round((evicted or 0) / 1e6, 1),

   103	\section{Related Work}
   104	\label{sec:related}
   105	
   106	\paragraph{Admission and scheduling for agentic serving.} Recent systems
   107	report large gains from treating an agent session, rather than a request, as
   108	the schedulable unit, and from preserving session state across tool calls:
   109	session-aware admission with CPU-restorable KV
   110	\cite{talaria,continuum}, program-level pause/resume with shortest-context-first
   111	restoration \cite{thunderagent}, and congestion-based control over an
   112	agent-level window \cite{concur}. A recurring measurement in this line is that
   113	recomputation, not admission, dominates: \cite{concur} attributes 49.1\% of
   114	end-to-end latency to recomputation, and \cite{mori} preserves state on the
   115	CPU at the cost of a reload. Our results are consistent with this literature
   116	rather than in tension with it, and \S\ref{sec:discussion} explains why: the
   117	mechanism these systems add is one our controller already has.
   118	
   119	\paragraph{Engine-side techniques.} A separate line improves the engine
   120	rather than the admission decision. Iteration-level batching and paged KV
   238	close together.
   239	
   240	\subsection{What the two mechanisms explain, arm by arm}
   241	\label{sec:results:attribution}
   242	
   243	We have offered two mechanisms, and the honest thing is to say what each one
   244	covers. For every controller arm we took the static cap closest to it in wall
   245	clock and compared their occupancy and their step cost. An arm is attributed to
   246	the unused-memory mechanism if it runs meaningfully fewer requests than its
   247	comparison, and to the step-cost mechanism if it runs a comparable number at a
   248	measurably higher cost. Everything else is a residual.
   249	
   250	\begin{center}
   251	\small
   252	\setlength{\tabcolsep}{4pt}
   253	\begin{tabular}{lrrrl}
   254	  \toprule
   255	  Arm & Occ. & $\overline{R}$ & $\tau_1$ & Attribution \\
   256	  \midrule
   257	  \texttt{horizon}=1  & 57.8 & 1.524 & 29.2 & residual \\
   258	  \sys{} base         & 49.2 & 1.353 & 28.7 & residual \\
   259	  \texttt{horizon}=2  & 54.7 & 1.439 & 33.6 & step cost \\
   260	  \texttt{horizon}=0  & 64.1 & 1.666 & 35.7 & step cost \\
   261	  \texttt{bres}       & 56.9 & 1.481 & 34.1 & step cost \\
   262	  \texttt{breshz1}    & 61.4 & 1.632 &  --- & residual \\
   263	  no gate, no resv.   & 62.1 & 1.678 &  --- & unused mem. \\
   264	  running gate$=$4    & 62.3 & 1.689 &  --- & residual \\
   265	  all throttles off   & 39.2 & 1.675 &  --- & residual \\
   266	  AIMD, retuned       & 66.6 & 1.706 &  --- & residual \\
   267	  AIMD, paper params  & 40.3 & 1.357 &  --- & residual \\
   268	  \bottomrule
   269	\end{tabular}
   270	\end{center}
    96	\subsection{Contributions}
    97	
    98	\begin{itemize}
    99	  \item \textbf{We identify the memory tier as what decides whether added
   100	        concurrency is paid for in recomputation, and therefore whether
   101	        admission control has any room.} Prefix reuse has a ceiling set by the
   102	        workload; whether that ceiling is realized is set by the tier.
   103	        Device-only, reuse collapses from 69.8\% to 27.4\% of the prompt
   104	        between $\overline{R}_{\text{busy}}$ 1.00 and 1.84 and the shortfall
   105	        reappears as recomputation, 8.6\,M to 20.7\,M tokens; with a host tier
   106	        the ceiling holds, 69.8\% to 68.1\% over 1.25 to 1.73, while the
   107	        \emph{device} misses more (\S\ref{sec:model}). Two configurations at
   108	        the same running count and the same pool occupancy realize 59.6\% and
   109	        68.8\% of the ceiling, so it is not how full the pool is but what
   110	        happens when it overflows. The per-step elasticity follows the same
   111	        line: it passes one above $\overline{R}_{\text{busy}} \approx 1.5$
   112	        device-only and stays below one across the controllers' whole
   113	        operating range with a host tier (\S\ref{sec:criterion}). Two
   114	        measurements that do not share a code path---client-side step timing
   115	        and engine-side token accounting---give the same answer, which is why
   116	        we state the criterion as a property of the workload \emph{and} the
   117	        configuration. This is the finding we would carry to another system
   118	        first, and it is what the title's question turns on.

   140	the base configuration, and the median number of sessions queueing for
   141	admission is 66. The configuration that finishes fastest---the repeat of that same static cap
   142	of four, at 209.1 minutes---runs at 71.9\%, and its first run at 70.2\%. Occupancy
   143	and running requests track each other reliably across the arms we have (the two
   144	columns in \texttt{occupancy.csv} rank the twenty arms almost identically), but
   145	running count does \emph{not} order wall clock: \texttt{horizon}=2 runs 1.25
   146	requests against the base arm's 1.14 and finishes 15 minutes slower. That gap is
   147	what the next subsection is about.
   148	
   149	The controller believes it is nearly full. Its own decision log records the
   150	projection it admits against---the largest of measured occupancy and the summed
   151	contexts of live sessions, plus a reserve for their growth---at a median of
   152	89{,}073 tokens against a budget of 96{,}360, i.e.\ 7\% free. Its measured
   153	occupancy at the same instants is 49{,}875 tokens: 48.2\% of that budget, or
   154	50.8\% of the pool, is idle. The
   155	projection is 1.79$\times$ the truth on this configuration. Across the
   156	seventeen controller runs we have projection logs for, the ratio runs from 1.24
   157	to 4.11 with a median of 1.59 (\texttt{projection.csv}), so 1.79 is this arm's
   158	value, not a constant of the design; the set is named here because the median
   159	moves with it---over every run in that file it is 1.40. Two terms produce the gap: charging every
   160	live session its full context, when a parked session's KV is evictable and
   161	therefore absent from the engine's count, and the growth reserve, which alone
   162	is 26{,}762 tokens, 28\% of the budget.
   163	
   164	Two things follow, and we are careful about the second.
   165	
   166	The first is that the controllers under-use the pool, and that this is a
   167	property of their estimates rather than of the hardware: the base
   168	configuration holds nearly half the pool idle (48.2\% of its budget, 50.8\% of\
   169	the pool) with 66 sessions queued, because
   170	it believes it has 7\% free when it has 48\%. Any account of why it is slow
   171	that does not mention this is incomplete.
   172	
   173	We then asked what actually stops it, by instrumenting the admission decision.
   174	The path is a conjunction: a session is authorized only if the SLO valve is
   175	open, the round gate is not full, and its projected footprint fits what remains
   176	of the budget. Over the base run's 8{,}196 blocked cycles---cycles with a
   177	session waiting and none admitted---the valve accounts for 6.8\%, the round gate
   178	for none, and a projection already above budget for 38.4\%.
   179	
   180	The remaining 54.8\% are cycles where the controller selected no candidate at
   310	\emph{Co-tenancy on the measurement host.} Our runs share a host with other
   311	tenants and with each other, and late in the campaign we found that this
   312	matters more than the pool guard catches. An audit
   313	(\texttt{gen\_concurrency.py}, data in \texttt{paper/data/concurrency.csv})
   314	reconstructs, for every run, how many other runs were alive for how long. Runs
   315	that overlapped three or more others for at least an hour have a per-step cost
   316	inflated by 25--40\%: the per-request decode interval $\tau_2$, which is
   317	measured client-side and never touches the sampler, sits at 30.9--33.3\,ms for
   318	those runs against 23.8--25.7\,ms for the same arms measured alone. Two
   319	controls pin the cause to co-tenancy rather than to the configuration. One
   320	run (\texttt{cap1}) holds $\tau_2$ at 20.3--20.7\,ms for its first five and a
   321	half hours and jumps to 22.4--27.5\,ms in the minute two other runs start. And
   322	two independent runs (\texttt{F:t85}, \texttt{F:rl99}) step down from
   323	$\approx$30\,ms to 22--25\,ms in the same wall-clock minute, when a third run
   324	ends. Twelve of our runs fall in the affected class. Eight are arms we do not
   325	report here; four are controller variants in Table~\ref{tab:runs}
   326	(\texttt{F:bres}, \texttt{F:hz0}, \texttt{F:hz2}, \texttt{F:breshz1}), and
   327	those four have been re-measured under the same one-run-per-GPU discipline as
   328	everything else. We report the episode because the failure is silent in a way
   329	worth knowing about: the engine-side counters---running count, pool occupancy,
   330	evictions, recomputation---are indistinguishable between the affected runs and
   331	the clean ones. The contaminated runs' \emph{volume} measurements are
   332	unaffected: the four affected host-tier arms reuse 0.691--0.694 of the prompt,
   333	inside the 0.681--0.698 range of the clean host-tier arms, so only their timing
   334	columns are unusable.
   335

   146	
   147	\paragraph{Prefix caching.} The prefix cache is often proposed as the signal
   148	an admission policy should use. In our workload it is not a policy variable:
   149	round-1+ hit rate is a function of the admission policy, and of the host tier,
   150	in a way an earlier draft of this paper got wrong in both directions. Without
   151	HiCache it collapses as the cap rises: 80.8\%, 78.5\%, 70.6\%, 56.0\%, 32.8\%
   152	for caps of one through five, against a planned 80.9\% for the trace. With
   153	HiCache it does not: 80.7\%, 79.6\%, 78.7\% for caps of two, three and four.
   154	Our controller sits at 80.6\%.
   155	So the honest statement is not that admission does not move this quantity---%
   156	\texttt{cap4} alone moves it 25 points---but that the host tier is what keeps
   157	it from moving, which is the same mechanism \S\ref{sec:model} and
   158	\S\ref{sec:criterion} keep running into. A configuration without a host tier
   159	that admits four sessions does not have the cache that a host-tier one at the
   160	same concurrency does. (Both numbers are the
   161	unweighted mean of the per-step planned hit rate over rounds 1 and later; the
   162	token-weighted figure for the same steps is 86.2\%, and the all-steps figure
   163	is 73.8\%. We use the unweighted round-1+ convention throughout, and flag it
   164	because the paper's earlier drafts mixed conventions.) We note the boundary of this claim---we varied admission policy,
    65	concurrency buys.
    66	
    67	The reason $\tau$ moves is recomputation, and whether recomputation happens is
    68	decided by the memory tier rather than by the policy. A session's prefix is
    69	worth reusing only while it is still resident, so prefix \emph{reuse} has a
    70	ceiling set by the workload---in our trace, 19.9\,M of the 28.5\,M prompt
    71	tokens, the most any configuration in our data reuses. Whether that ceiling is
    72	realized is set by the tier. With the KV pool on the device alone, reuse
    73	collapses as concurrency rises---69.8\% at $\overline{R}_{\text{busy}}$ 1.00
    74	down to 27.4\% at 1.84---and the shortfall reappears one for one as
    75	recomputation, 8.6\,M to 20.7\,M tokens. With a host tier in front of it, the
    76	same ceiling holds across the range our controllers occupy---69.8\% to 68.1\%
    77	between 1.25 and 1.73---even though the \emph{device} misses more. Two
    78	configurations at the same running count and the same pool occupancy realize
    79	59.6\% and 68.8\% of the ceiling respectively (\S\ref{sec:model}); what
    80	separates them is not how full the pool is but what happens when it overflows.
    81	That is the finding we would carry to another system first, and it is why this
    82	paper's answer to its own title is a property of the deployment rather than of

codex
## Major issues

1. **四条受同机争用影响的 Table I 结果仍被当作有效实验。** 审计的分类数量是对的：`concurrency.csv` 有 123 条 run，其中 12 条 `CONTESTED`、4 条 `watch`、107 条 `clean`；Table I 中受影响的确是 `bres`、`hz0`、`hz2`、`breshz1` 四条，逐请求解码间隔为 30.9–33.3 ms（[concurrency.csv](/root/paper/foyer/paper/data/concurrency.csv:105)；[发现记录](/root/paper/foyer/docs/finding_concurrency_contamination_20260924.md:81)）。但 §repro 写“**those four have been re-measured**”（[sec_discussion.tex](/root/paper/foyer/paper/sec_discussion.tex:324)）；当前 `results/night/` 中没有这四条对应的 `_r2` 目录及 `summary.json`，而 [runs.csv](/root/paper/foyer/paper/data/runs.csv:23) 和 [Table I](/root/paper/foyer/paper/sec_results.tex:118) 仍列旧值。若重测尚在运行，这句话是提前宣布完成。争用可能改变墙钟、SLO 和前沿距离，因而“九个变体均未胜出”的完整判决目前不能由表中数据支持。发现记录本身也承认 `bres` 重测后可能从明显落后变为贴近前沿（[发现记录](/root/paper/foyer/docs/finding_concurrency_contamination_20260924.md:104)）。

2. **污染已进入机制归因及消融结论。** `hz2`、`hz0`、`bres` 在归因表中被归为“step cost”，依据正是受争用抬高的 τ₁（[sec_results.tex](/root/paper/foyer/paper/sec_results.tex:240)）。`bres` 的 31.3 ms τ₂、慢 4.2 分钟和低 6.0 个百分点又被用来否定改记账底座的路线（[sec_results.tex](/root/paper/foyer/paper/sec_results.tex:280)）。这些结果在干净重测完成前均不能作该因果解释。§repro 说“只有 timing columns unusable”（[sec_discussion.tex](/root/paper/foyer/paper/sec_discussion.tex:328)）也没有落实到正文分析。

3. **“工作负载设上限、内存层决定兑现”有实质支持，但现在的表述过于绝对。** 各百分比和体积可追到 [reuse.csv](/root/paper/foyer/paper/data/reuse.csv:10)：设备侧 69.8→27.4%、重算 8.6→20.7M；host 侧 69.8→68.1%；cap3 对照 59.6% 与 68.8%。不过论文把 **19.9M 定义为“数据中最多复用的量”，再称其为工作负载上限**（[sec_model.tex](/root/paper/foyer/paper/sec_model.tex:197)；[gen_reuse.py](/root/paper/foyer/scripts/lab/gen_reuse.py:63)），这样呈现有循环性。它并非不可检验：从 `data/replay_pois200_l04.csv` 独立求和，round≥1 的 `prefix_len` 为 **19,964,074** tokens，prompt 总量为 **28,526,871**；这才是应说明定义及假设的 trace 上界，按一位小数约为 **20.0M**。此外，同一设备侧配置在 cap1 已接近上限，cap5 却远离它，因此兑现程度显然还取决于准入造成的交错与运行数；host tier 只在已测的 1.25–1.73 区间保持接近上限（[sec_model.tex](/root/paper/foyer/paper/sec_model.tex:210)）。[摘要](/root/paper/foyer/paper/main.tex:48)和[引言](/root/paper/foyer/paper/sec_intro.tex:67)中的二分断语应收窄。

4. **cap3 对照有价值，但尚非隔离的 tier 实验。** 两条 run 使用相同 trace 哈希、静态 cap、代码哈希及 101,432-token 设备池；然而 `cap3_fixed` 于 9 月 21 日在 GPU 2 运行，`cap3_hc` 于 9 月 22 日在 GPU 3 运行（[前者 metadata](/root/paper/foyer/results/night/pois200_cap3_fixed/metadata.json:2)；[后者 metadata](/root/paper/foyer/results/night/pois200_cap3_hc/metadata.json:2)）。不同 GPU、日期、其他租户负载、GPU 时钟或主机内存争用尚未由配对或交叉重复排除。两者**平均**设备占用 55.8% 与 55.5%（[occupancy.csv](/root/paper/foyer/paper/data/occupancy.csv:7)）也不保证占用峰值、驻留时间和驱逐顺序相同。因此“差别就是溢出后的 tier 行为”是合理机制解释，尚不是该对照单独证明的因果结论。

5. **“59.6% 和 68.8% 的上限”算术错误。** 这两个数是**占总 prompt 的复用比例**，不是占 19.9M 上限的比例；对应的上限兑现比例约为 **17.0/19.9＝85.4%** 和 **19.6/19.9＝98.5%**（[reuse.csv](/root/paper/foyer/paper/data/reuse.csv:12)）。错误贯穿[摘要](/root/paper/foyer/paper/main.tex:53)、[引言](/root/paper/foyer/paper/sec_intro.tex:77)及[模型节](/root/paper/foyer/paper/sec_model.tex:243)。

6. **尚未完成的预测被写成已有测试。** 模型节的原句是：“**the reuse collapse should move to roughly three times the running count on the host-tier family---beyond any operating point our controllers reach, but within reach of a static cap. \S\ref{sec:results} reports the test.**”（[sec_model.tex](/root/paper/foyer/paper/sec_model.tex:252)）。当前没有 cap5_hc/cap6_hc/cap8_hc 的 200-session 结果，§results 也未报告此项测试。容量约 3 倍直接推出运行数约 3 倍，还隐含 KV 需求随运行数线性增加；异质会话下需要实测支持。

7. **两种命中率口径被混同。** 新 reuse 值是“所有步骤、按 prompt token 加权”，Related Work 的 80.8→32.8% 与 80.7→78.7% 是 round≥1 的逐步非加权均值。模型节说“两种口径 **differ only in the denominator**”不成立：筛选的步骤和加权方式都变了（[sec_model.tex](/root/paper/foyer/paper/sec_model.tex:235)）。Related Work 又把随策略变化的数称为“**per-step planned hit rate**”，并说“**We use the unweighted round-1+ convention throughout**”（[sec_discussion.tex](/root/paper/foyer/paper/sec_discussion.tex:147)），与本轮新口径直接冲突；真正的 planned rate 是 trace 输入，不能随 cap 从 80.8% 降到 32.8%。这里至少混了“planned/observed”及两种归一化。

## Minor issues

- [sec_discussion.tex](/root/paper/foyer/paper/sec_discussion.tex:225)仍说两条 `cap3+HiCache` 中一条超 cap、故不作重复；[sec_results.tex](/root/paper/foyer/paper/sec_results.tex:24)及[frontier.py](/root/paper/foyer/scripts/lab/frontier.py:13)已明确更正：超 cap 的是**无 HiCache**的另一条 run，`cap3_hc_r2` 是有效前沿锚点。这是重构留下的直接矛盾。
- “复用短缺一对一变成重算”（[sec_model.tex](/root/paper/foyer/paper/sec_model.tex:224)）是 `reuse = prompt − recompute` 的记账恒等式，不能独立验证机制；更有信息量的是设备驱逐量与未缓存 prefill 的接近关系（[eviction.csv](/root/paper/foyer/paper/data/eviction.csv:10)）。相关段落宜区分定义式和独立观测。
- “12 条受影响 run 的 τ₂ 均膨胀 25–40%”说得过满。12 是**满足并发阈值的分类数**；其中 `t85`、`t90`、`var_foyer` 的整段 τ₂ 并未显示同样膨胀（[concurrency.csv](/root/paper/foyer/paper/data/concurrency.csv:89)；[concurrency.csv](/root/paper/foyer/paper/data/concurrency.csv:119)）。并发是风险标记，不能等同于每条 run 的已测损害。
- 旧撤回主张中，错误的 tool wait 中位数推论、空转率单调性、记账双算均有明确纠正；驱逐→重算解释也有新的 `uncached` 证据，不能简单算作无证据复活。但 Related Work 的“**it is not a policy variable**”紧接“**is a function of the admission policy**”（[sec_discussion.tex](/root/paper/foyer/paper/sec_discussion.tex:147)）自相矛盾，容易复活旧的“命中率与准入无关”说法；这也与[撤回记录](/root/paper/foyer/CLAUDE.md:31)所要求的证据纪律不符。
- `reuse.csv` 先把 token 数四舍五入到 0.1M 再计算百分比（[gen_reuse.py](/root/paper/foyer/scripts/lab/gen_reuse.py:78)）。因此数字可追到 CSV，但并非总能从原始计数按同一位数重现；例如 cap1 原始日志为 19,934,847 / 28,526,871 ≈ **69.9%**，表中为 **69.8%**。宜统一从未舍入计数计算比例。

## Questions requiring clarification

1. 四条污染 run 的干净重测目前各到什么状态？请给出 `_r2` 的 `summary.json`、完整步数、并发审计及更新后的 Table I；否则应撤回“已重测”和依赖这四行的判决。
2. 19.9M 究竟是“样本中观测最大值”，还是按 trace 前缀结构定义的理论上界？若为后者，请给公式，并解释它与独立求得的 19,964,074 tokens 及计划命中率口径的关系。
3. 能否在同一 GPU 上交叉、重复 cap3_fixed 与 cap3_hc，并同时报告 host 读回量、驱逐轨迹和 GPU/主机争用记录？这会检验 matched-occupancy 对照的因果解释。
4. 污染组“体积不受影响”的证据目前只是 0.691–0.694 落在干净组 0.681–0.698 的宽范围内（[sec_discussion.tex](/root/paper/foyer/paper/sec_discussion.tex:328)）。争用可改变请求完成顺序、会话交错和缓存驱逐，因此这个范围重叠不足以证明不变。请比较**同一策略的干净重测**在每轮/每会话的复用、host 命中、驱逐及重算分布。

## Acceptance assessment

**以当前稿件状态估计：约 5–15%。** 这是审稿判断，不是某刊录用率预测。负结果、完整的静态前沿和主动披露失败实验有研究价值；但主结论仍有上限定义与因果归属问题，四条 Table I 数据已知受污染却仍用于判决和机制归因，“已重测”与现有产物不符，预测又被写成已报告测试。这些问题直接影响核心证据链。单一 GPU、单一模型和单一 trace，以及很少的有效重复，则进一步限制了投向 CCF-B／CAS Q2 时的说服力。
tokens used
111,591
## Major issues

1. **四条受同机争用影响的 Table I 结果仍被当作有效实验。** 审计的分类数量是对的：`concurrency.csv` 有 123 条 run，其中 12 条 `CONTESTED`、4 条 `watch`、107 条 `clean`；Table I 中受影响的确是 `bres`、`hz0`、`hz2`、`breshz1` 四条，逐请求解码间隔为 30.9–33.3 ms（[concurrency.csv](/root/paper/foyer/paper/data/concurrency.csv:105)；[发现记录](/root/paper/foyer/docs/finding_concurrency_contamination_20260924.md:81)）。但 §repro 写“**those four have been re-measured**”（[sec_discussion.tex](/root/paper/foyer/paper/sec_discussion.tex:324)）；当前 `results/night/` 中没有这四条对应的 `_r2` 目录及 `summary.json`，而 [runs.csv](/root/paper/foyer/paper/data/runs.csv:23) 和 [Table I](/root/paper/foyer/paper/sec_results.tex:118) 仍列旧值。若重测尚在运行，这句话是提前宣布完成。争用可能改变墙钟、SLO 和前沿距离，因而“九个变体均未胜出”的完整判决目前不能由表中数据支持。发现记录本身也承认 `bres` 重测后可能从明显落后变为贴近前沿（[发现记录](/root/paper/foyer/docs/finding_concurrency_contamination_20260924.md:104)）。
